Motivation: 长生命周期KV缓存在未来查询出现之前就必须被压缩，因此无法通过观测注意力（如H2O、SnapKV的做法）来判断token的重要性——这类方法在NoPE MLA模型上检索准确率跌至0.00-0.33。作者旨在寻找一种不依赖query即可判定的逐出信号，使压缩在查询未知时依然可靠。

Method: VestigeKV利用Kimi Linear的NoPE MLA模型中64维解耦分支——被识别为RoPE在NoPE训练下被重新改造出的显著性通道。算法仅读取每行11%的数据，据此将缓存分区：top-m行保留在"被关注层"，其余行精确地（而非删除）移入GPU驻留的归档层，归档层通过认证触发器按步可达。方法无需训练、量化或改动权重/内核，并提供主机卸载的VRAM回收变体。

Result: 在8k至65k上下文下，8x压缩检索准确率保持1.00，32x压缩为0.92，与全行选择无差距。被关注层在32x时仅占每token缓存（8.1KB）中的0.25KB；归档层保持bit-exact且GPU驻留。标准"召回层"配置在128x仍保持1.00。在RoPE MLA上同样的操作会崩溃至0.08（纯逐出为0.42），证明该方法仅适用于NoPE模型。所有阈值在数据之前冻结，并附带20个归档验证结果与8条封闭路线。

Conclusion: VestigeKV证明：在NoPE架构中，可通过缓存自身携带的查询无关信号进行精确的、零开销的KV缓存压缩，且不依赖观测注意力。该方法的有效性本质上依赖于NoPE训练将解耦分支重塑为显著性通道（RoPE下top-1目标占比2.3-6.7% vs 10.2-46.8%，且查询无关精确合并在RoPE下可证明不可能）。作者谨慎声明此方法仅适用于Kimi Linear，虽然推测可扩展到使用NoPE Gated-MLA且缓存布局匹配的Kimi K3，但不做超出实测范围的主张。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [139] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的标签移除方法，作为模块化输出过滤器近似重训练模型的后移除置信向量，避免特征空间调整或损失函数收敛，仅需已有标签和先验输出置信度即可工作，在多个分类任务上达到与全量重训练相当的性能，同时提升计算效率和隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类系统分类法演化的场景中标签移除频繁发生，模型需随之适应。现有方法（重训练式与特征空间调整式）普遍存在依赖原始数据、计算与存储开销大、结果不一致、可扩展性差以及模型效用下降等共同局限。亟需一种不依赖原始数据且高效的方法来应对标签移除任务。

Method: 提出一种利用输出空间统计重分布的新方法，通过近似重训练模型在标签移除后的置信度向量来实现。该方法作为模块化输出过滤器应用，绕过了特征空间调整或损失函数收敛的负担，缓解可扩展性限制；只需已有的标签和先前的输出置信度，不依赖具体数据，从而减轻隐私问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个分类任务上与全量重训练的性能相当，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面有显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的输出空间统计重分布方法是一种有效、高效且隐私友好的标签移除方案，能够作为模块化过滤器与现有分类系统集成，在保持模型效用的同时解决了现有方法在数据依赖、计算成本和可扩展性方面的核心缺陷。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [140] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RobustSeiz 是一个开源的、模型无关的框架，为癫痫发作检测器提供标准化且可复现的压力测试协议，在部署前通过受控的、临床动机驱动的分布偏移（环境、噪声、对抗性变换）来评估检测器的鲁棒性。该框架将四个公共头皮脑电数据集标准化为BIDS-EEG格式，提供Docker化GPU流水线、实验注册表及完整的评估指标（灵敏度、精度、F1、假阳性率、起病时间偏移、蒙特卡洛dropout一致性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管癫痫发作检测器在held-out脑电数据上表现良好，但在真实世界的采集变异、伪迹和对抗性输入下可能失效。现有研究缺乏标准化、可复现的协议来系统评估检测器在部署前面对临床相关干扰的鲁棒性，这阻碍了模型之间的公平比较和临床安全部署。

Method: RobustSeiz提供模型无关的鲁棒性评估框架：(1) 将CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena和SeizeIT1四个公共头皮脑电语料库标准化为BIDS-EEG树结构；(2) 在held-out划分上评估受试者无关的检测器；(3) 在预定义超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗性变换；(4) 每次运行报告样本级与事件级的灵敏度、精度、F1、每24小时假阳性率、Lead/Lag起病时间以及蒙特卡洛dropout预测一致性；(5) 提供Docker化GPU流水线、实验注册表以及完整评估和研究子集两种模式。

Result: 作者在TUSZ数据集上使用当代癫痫检测器演示了该框架在完整变换网格上的运行；AWGN（加性高斯白噪声）分析展示了扰动严重程度如何影响检测质量、起病时间偏移和预测一致性，验证了框架能够量化不同临床压力源下检测器性能的退化模式。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为评估癫痫检测器在真实临床压力源下的鲁棒性提供了共享的基准标准，将部署前评估从仅关注干净数据上的准确率扩展到了对临床多样性和对抗性条件下系统行为的系统化检验，有助于缩小实验室性能与临床实际性能之间的差距。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [141] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散增强LLM（Uno），将标准自回归（AR）LLM与轻量扩散采样相结合，通过扩散蒸馏学习多token并行生成能力，实现无损加速（最高3×）并可对现有开源AR模型进行增强，在多种任务上超越更大规模的扩散LLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的成功依赖于next-token prediction（NTP），但其自回归（AR）结构需要逐个顺序生成token，导致生成速度慢。现有加速方法如投机解码需要单独的草稿模型，而扩散LLM（d-LLMs）则牺牲了底层AR模型的质量。因此需要一种既能保持AR模型质量又能实现并行、快速生成的方案。

Method: 将模型参数解耦为两组：AR权重（使用标准NTP目标训练）和轻量扩散权重（用于并行生成多个token）。扩散权重通过简单的扩散蒸馏（Diffusion Distillation）阶段学习，对现有LLM训练流程几乎无额外开销。同时引入Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持在固定上下文长度下的无损加速和推理时扩展。模型可从零训练或通过增强现有开源AR权重构建。

Result: Uno在各类batch size下吞吐量均超过领先的投机解码方法，相对基础AR模型最高加速3×（包括在设备支持的最大batch size下）。8B规模Uno在智能体工具使用、编程和长上下文推理等基准上超越26B的DiffusionGemma和专有的Mercury 2。

Conclusion: Uno结合了AR模型的生成质量与扩散模型的并行采样效率，无需草稿模型即可实现无损加速，同时可灵活地从零训练或增强现有开源模型，展示了扩散蒸馏作为提升LLM推理效率的有效范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [142] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种位置不变的极端分位数处理效应（QTE）估计方法。针对重尾潜在结果，现有外推估计器在共同位置平移下不具不变性，作者将位置不变的Fraga极端值指数估计器通过逆倾向得分加权适配到因果框架，并用差分式外推方案代替原公式使位置参数在分位数差中抵消。证明了估计量的相合性和渐近正态性，并提供一致方差估计器以支持渐近有效的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端分位数处理效应（QTE）在目标分位数远超出数据范围的应用中具有核心价值（如金融、保险等领域）。对于重尾潜在结果，现有极端QTE估计器依赖外推结合因果极端值指数（EVI）估计，但其在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时不具不变性，而总体QTE本身是位置不变的。这一缺陷导致估计结果对位置参数敏感，从而影响其可靠性和解释性。

Method: 作者分两步解决：第一步，将位置不变的Fraga EVI估计器通过逆倾向得分加权（IPSW）适配到因果框架中；第二步，将原始外推公式替换为差分式方案，使得取分位数差时位置参数自然抵消。由此得到的QTE估计器具备位置不变性，并建立了一致性与渐近正态性理论，同时提供了一致方差估计器以支持统计推断。

Result: 通过模拟研究验证了所提方法的三个关键性质：位置不变性、对阈值选择的稳定性以及合理的置信区间覆盖率。理论上证明了估计量的相合性和渐近正态性，并给出了一致方差估计。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了极端QTE估计中位置不变性缺失的重要问题，提出了一个新的位置不变的极端QTE估计框架，兼具理论保证和实证稳定性，为重尾数据下极端分位数处理效应的可靠推断提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [143] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，将主动偏好学习建模为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，通过低维参数子空间中的扩展卡尔曼滤波实现奖励模型的不确定性追踪，在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上相比其他贝叶斯深度学习方法展现出更优的采样效率、运行时间、可扩展性与校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF是学习奖励模型的有效方法但样本效率低下，使得主动学习成为关键。然而，大型神经网络奖励模型的有效不确定性量化仍是核心挑战，而全参数空间的后验推断计算代价过高。

Method: 将主动偏好学习构建为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题；在低维参数子空间内通过扩展卡尔曼滤波进行序贯推断，而非对完整网络参数空间做后验推断；随新偏好查询到达持续更新奖励模型后验；可扩展地采样神经网络参数以高效计算主动奖励学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，该方法在采样效率、运行时间、可扩展性及校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法；学习所得奖励模型产生了具有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF中基于偏好的奖励建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [144] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对条件生成模型中梯度函数（score function）在奇异扩散系数下可能失效的问题，提出利用因果最优传输（causal optimal transport）定义近似损失函数，在最小假设下识别用于引导的最小熵控制。其核心依赖因果最优传输及其与（条件）扩散过程可预测表示性质的刻画，该刻画借用了Üstünel关于鞅问题适定性的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件生成模型在训练中严重依赖梯度函数（score function）进行引导，但当生成模型是具有奇异扩散系数的扩散过程，且底层（条件）密度不存在或不光滑时，基于梯度的传统方法将失效。因此需要在更弱的假设下设计可行的损失函数来实现引导。

Method: 利用因果最优传输理论定义近似损失函数，通过因果最优传输与扩散过程可预测表示性质的等价刻画来构造最小熵控制，从而替代传统基于梯度函数的引导方式。该方法依赖于（条件）扩散过程鞅问题适定性（à la Üstünel）。

Result: 在密度不存在或非光滑以及扩散系数奇异的情况下，所提出的方法仍能识别出用于引导的最小熵控制，且在最小假设下成立，从而扩展了条件生成模型引导方法的适用范围。

Conclusion: 因果最优传输为奇异扩散条件下的生成模型引导提供了理论框架，利用可预测表示性质可在弱正则性假设下实现最小熵控制，弥补了传统score-based方法的局限。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [145] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct-P方法，针对FP4张量核心在注意力计算中因softmax转换开销和片上依赖而无法自动提速的问题。该方法通过将分数直接映射为FP4概率（用于非因果推理），以及将正向量化直接传递到反向传播（用于因果路径），在NVIDIA GB200上实现高达2.13倍于BF16的前向吞吐，并将单GPU 8B参数完整更新加速至1.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell架构的4位浮点(FP4)张量核心虽然能加速矩阵乘法，但当矩阵乘积规模缩小后，softmax转换开销和片上依赖成为瓶颈，导致注意力机制无法自动获得加速。此外，在分布式训练中直接使用低精度（MXFP4）表示概率和值会导致训练发散，亟需解决低精度注意力计算中的数值稳定性问题。

Method: 提出Direct-P方法，包含两条路径：(1) 非因果推理路径：将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率，避免昂贵的softmax转换开销；(2) 因果路径：将前向量化直接传递到反向传播中，通过保存量化的查询和键来重建概率，并使用8位浮点(FP8)作为梯度操作数。在分布式训练场景下，保留FP8概率和值以确保训练收敛。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB200上，非因果前向推理吞吐量达到BF16的2.13倍；完整的单GPU 8B参数更新在因果路径下加速达1.14倍。分布式训练中所有测试的MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散，而匹配的FP8方案保持稳定收敛。

Conclusion: Direct-P证明了通过设计感知FP4张量核心特性的注意力算法，可以绕过softmax转换和片上依赖瓶颈，实现有效加速。同时，FP8是分布式低精度注意力训练的可行精度选择，而MXFP4在该场景下不具备数值稳定性。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [146] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非耦合学习算法HOOD（高阶乐观折扣算法），适用于任意N人有限博弈（每人至多K个动作），在任意时间范围内将个体遗憾统一控制在O(N^3 log² K)量级。该算法是乐观FTRL的变体，结合了折扣(N+1)阶预测器与经过"提升"的策略空间上的熵正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾（常数纳什均衡收敛率）是一个长期未解决的难题。以往尝试的主要障碍在于：普通OptFTRL算法在一般博弈中会引发策略序列的大幅振荡，阻碍了常数遗憾的达成。作者旨在通过设计受控的阻尼机制来消除这一关键障碍。

Method: 论文提出HOOD算法，其核心设计包括：（1）采用折扣的(N+1)阶预测器，即超越常规一阶/二阶的更高阶乐观预测，利用过去多个时间步的信息来预测对手行为；（2）对博弈策略空间进行适当的"提升"（lifting），在增广空间上施加熵正则化；（3）通过折扣因子控制高阶历史信息的权重，以受控方式抑制策略序列的大幅振荡。

Result: HOOD算法在任意N人正规博弈中实现了O(N^3 log² K)的个体遗憾上界，该界在游戏时间范围上保持一致（不随T增长），实现了常数遗憾。与同期独立工作（Liu, Farina & Ozdaglar）得到的O(N^21 log^4 K)相比，本文的界在N和K维度上均显著更优。

Conclusion: 本文通过高阶乐观预测与折扣机制的巧妙结合，首次以多项式且较紧的依赖关系解决了非耦合学习算法在一般博弈中的常数遗憾问题，为博弈论中的学习动力学研究提供了新的理论工具与视角。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [147] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs），一种受生物学启发的复值时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特殊情况），并引入参数无关的两抽头前瞻性输入修正，解决深层连续时间循环网络中时间积分导致的自底向上信号延迟与自顶向下误差衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分赋予连续时间循环网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中会延迟自底向上信号并衰减自顶向下误差，这一失效模式阻碍了深层循环网络的有效训练。

Method: 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是一通带滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。通过参数无关的两抽头更新使每层自底向上输入前瞻化，不改变循环递推和平行扫描结构。将该修正扩展到一般对角SSM，并缓解时间梯度截断（仅空间反向传播）时深度依赖的梯度衰减。

Result: 在前瞻变体中，RQFs、S5和ORGaNICs（非线性门控RNN）在全BPTT和仅空间反向传播下均匹配或超越非前瞻对照组。非残差宽度32的六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达到96.09%准确率；宽度64的六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs是参数高效的递归基底，前瞻输入编码是深度连续时间循环网络的输入侧修正方案，可有效缓解深层时间积分带来的信号延迟与梯度衰减问题。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [148] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个低成本、开源的端到端自动驾驶实验平台，结合微型Ackermann车辆、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生，支持从仿真到真实世界的可控实验。作为首个基线，实现命令条件行为克隆，在真实闭环实验中达到6.1cm平均横向误差（人类演示为4.7cm），在数字孪生中相机视场角从58°增至120°可将误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。合成数据+真实数据训练的高容量策略是唯一能完成全部四条赛道闭环的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究需要从仿真到真实世界的可复现桥梁，但现有平台往往成本高昂或难以连接仿真与实物实验。作者旨在提供一个低成本、开源的综合测试平台，使仿真方法能直接在真实微型车上验证，支持sim-to-real研究。

Method: 平台包含物理车辆、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹注册及Webots数字孪生。实现命令条件行为克隆：神经网络策略输入车载相机图像和高层导航命令，输出转向与速度。评估在真实车辆和仿真中均进行，并通过合成数据生成与sim-to-real图像转换降低外观差异，比较不同容量策略的训练配置。

Result: 真实闭环实验中，学习策略达到6.1cm平均横向误差，接近人类演示的4.7cm。数字孪生中，相机视场角从58°加宽到120°可将平均横向误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。结合合成数据与真实演示训练的高容量策略是唯一能在闭环中完成全部四条赛道路线的配置。

Conclusion: 该开源平台被证明是sim-to-real研究的实用测试平台，提供了命令条件模仿学习的初始基线，未来可支持更多端到端自动驾驶方法的可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [149] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了带转移不确定性的通用和并发随机博弈（CSGs）的PAC学习框架，解决了纳什均衡存在性挑战。算法维护基于数据的L1置信集并求解鲁棒CSG以计算社会福利最优的ε-NE，通过纳什边际刻画实现均衡存在性的原则性推理，要么返回ε-近似NE，要么给出不存在精确NE的可靠证书。样本复杂度为多项式阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用和并发随机博弈中，在转移核不确定下学习纳什均衡是一个关键但未被充分解决的问题。现有的学习框架往往回避或假设NE必然存在，而实际CSGs中NE的存在性本身就是一个难点。因此需要一种既能学习近似NE又能处理NE可能不存在的PAC框架。

Method: 算法维护数据驱动的L1置信集覆盖转移核，并求解鲁棒CSG以计算社会福利最优的ε-NE；利用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖；提出纳什边际（Nash margin）刻画，实现关于均衡存在性的原则性推理——若均衡存在则返回ε-NE且社会福利ε-接近最优，否则提供不存在精确NE的健全证书。

Result: 在相关状态-动作对满足最小可达性条件p_reach>0时，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max²H⁴|S|²|A|/(p_reach·ε²))。基准CSG实验表明近似最优性能、正确处理均衡存在/不存在，且样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将从MDP扩展到CSG的PAC学习推进到能处理均衡存在性不确定性的层面，为带不确定性的通用和随机博弈学习提供了首个端到端框架，兼具理论保障和实证验证。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Decoherence-controlled collective criticality in a two-dimensional quantum Stag Hunt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02957)
*Rajdeep Tah*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子博弈中的物理退相干如何在不影响微观战略中性的前提下改变相互作用种群的宏观热力学行为。以方格子上的EWL Stag Hunt博弈为例，将噪声两人支付矩阵精确映射为信道依赖的Ising参数，发现去极化信道可驱动精确的临界点，而相位阻尼保持在有序共存区，并揭示了两量子比特纠缠与集体相互作用强度是不同物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子博弈中微观量子特性（如战略中性、纠缠）与宏观统计力学行为之间的关系，探究物理退相干是否以及如何改变相互作用种群的相变与临界行为。已有工作多聚焦于量子博弈的微观策略分析，而对噪声环境下量子博弈嵌入空间结构后的集体热力学行为缺乏系统研究。

Method: 将EWL Stag Hunt博弈作为方格子上的独立最近邻相互作用嵌入，对三种退相干信道（相位阻尼、去极化、振幅阻尼）计算受限策略下的噪声两人支付矩阵，并将其精确映射为信道依赖的二维Ising模型参数J(Γ,p)和H(Γ,p)。采用蒙特卡洛有限尺寸标度分析验证临界性质。

Result: 相位阻尼与去极化信道共享微观中性分支H=0，但只有去极化以(1-p)^2方式抑制相互作用。β=1时去极化驱动精确的方格子临界点p*≈0.233460，而相位阻尼保持有序共存。振幅阻尼表现出强信道放置依赖性，其中性分支在p=1/3处消失。资源负性表明微观两比特纠缠与集体相互作用强度是相互独立的量。

Conclusion: 物理退相干可以在保持量子博弈微观战略中性的同时将对应的相互作用种群从有序共存推进到临界甚至无序区域，但延伸后的格子体系本质上仍是经典二维Ising系统，其临界行为服从标准Ising普适类。

Abstract: Physical decoherence can preserve the microscopic strategic neutrality condition of a quantum game while changing the thermodynamic regime of the corresponding interacting population. We demonstrate this for an Eisert--Wilkens--Lewenstein (\textit{EWL}) Stag Hunt embedded as independent nearest neighbor encounters on a square lattice. For the restricted strategies $\mathsf{Q}=i\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathsf{D}=i\mathbb{Y}$, noisy two-player payoff matrices are determined for phase damping, depolarization, and amplitude damping and mapped exactly to channel dependent Ising parameters $\mathfrak{J}(Γ,p)$ and $\mathfrak{H}(Γ,p)$. Phase damping and depolarization show the clearest contrast: they share the same microscopic neutrality branch $\mathfrak{H}=0$, while only depolarization suppresses the interaction as $(1-p)^2$. At $β=1$, this produces an exact depolarization-driven square-lattice critical point at $p_{*}\approx 0.233460\ldots$, whereas phase damping remains in the ordered coexistence regime along the same neutrality branch. Monte Carlo finite size scaling is consistent with two-dimensional Ising criticality and distinguishes field driven coexistence below $p_{*}$ from a smooth crossover above it. Amplitude damping additionally reveals a strong dependence on channel placement: the post-strategy neutrality branch reaches $Γ=0$ at $p=1/3$ and then disappears. Resource negativity further shows that microscopic two-qubit entanglement and collective interaction strength are distinct quantities. The resulting extended lattice remains an ordinary classical Ising system.

</details>


### [151] [Microscopically exact transport equation for the quantum Calogero model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03018)
*Vir B. Bulchandani,Fabian H. L. Essler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从微观第一性原理出发，为量子Calogero模型（即一维直线上逆平方相互作用的玻色子）推导出了精确的输运方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究逆平方相互作用的一维玻色子系统（Calogero模型）的输运性质，并希望从微观量子力学第一性原理出发建立精确的宏观输运描述，而非依赖近似或唯象模型。

Method: 从Calogero模型的微观量子力学描述出发，通过严格的数学推导，得到精确的输运方程。

Result: 成功推导出量子Calogero模型的精确输运方程。

Conclusion: 该研究为Calogero模型的输运行为提供了从微观第一性原理导出的精确理论框架，可作为后续深入研究该模型非平衡动力学的基础。

Abstract: We derive an exact transport equation for the quantum Calogero model, i.e. inverse-square interacting bosons on a line, from microscopic first principles.

</details>


### [152] [Classical dipolar Heisenberg models on the Archimedean and Laves lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02934)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了11种阿基米德晶格和8种Laves平面晶格（共计15种不同顶点集）上的经典偶极-偶极相互作用海森伯自旋系统。通过计算傅里叶空间相互作用矩阵、Luttinger-Tisza序波矢、经典基态以及线性自旋波色散等，发现基态分为三类：五个共线、两个非共线（角度与晶格对称性相称）、八个以超越角倾斜（精确到30位数字）。破缺Luttinger-Tisza强条件与不相称倾斜的出现一一对应；晶格是否倾斜由局域配位几何决定，而倾斜角度由长程尾部决定。研究结果构成了1-均匀平面铺砌及其对偶上偶极海森伯系统的谱图集，可为非弹性中子散射和人工纳米磁体阵列提供预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极-偶极相互作用的磁系统（如人工纳米磁体阵列、磁性离子晶格材料）在具有几何阻挫的晶格上表现出丰富多彩的基态和激发谱，但此前缺乏对完整平面均匀铺砌族系的系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，对全部阿基米德和Laves平面晶格进行统一的偶极海森伯系统谱学分析。

Method: 作者对每种晶格计算傅里叶空间的相互作用矩阵，提取Luttinger-Tisza序波矢，在相称磁性晶胞上进行无约束最小化确定经典基态，沿（磁性）布里渊区高对称路径计算线性化自旋波色散，并用Holstein-Primakoff方法计算磁矩缩减。通过截断相互作用范围来区分局域配位几何与长程尾部对倾斜行为的影响。

Result: 基态分为三类：五个共线基态、两个非共线基态（角度与晶格对称性相称）、八个以超越角倾斜的基态（确定到30位数字）。Luttinger-Tisza强条件失效恰与不相称倾斜出现完全对应。晶格是否倾斜由局域配位几何决定，倾斜角由长程尾部决定。量子修正由磁基大小调控，与阻挫不相关；角共享晶格拥有最狭窄的低能分支。

Conclusion: 本文构建了1-均匀平面铺砌及其对偶上偶极海森伯系统的谱图集，揭示了倾斜行为与Luttinger-Tisza条件的精确对应关系，证明了阻挫与涨落分类相互独立，并为非弹性中子散射实验和偶极耦合纳米磁体人工阵列提供了可检验的理论预言。

Abstract: We study classical Heisenberg spins interacting through the dipole--dipole interaction on the eleven Archimedean and eight Laves planar lattices at canonical geometry, which reduce to fifteen distinct vertex sets. For each lattice we compute the Fourier-space interaction matrix $\Am(\bk)$, extract the Luttinger--Tisza ordering wave vector $\bk_{0}$, determine the classical ground state by unconstrained minimization on the commensurate magnetic cell, and evaluate the linearized spin-wave dispersion $\varepsilon_{i}(\bk)$ along the high-symmetry path of the (magnetic) Brillouin zone together with the Holstein--Primakoff moment reduction. The ground states fall into three classes: five are collinear, two are non-collinear at angles commensurate with the lattice symmetry, and eight cant at transcendental angles, which we determine to thirty digits. Across the family, failure of the Luttinger--Tisza strong condition coincides exactly with the appearance of incommensurate canting, with no exceptions in either direction. Truncating the interaction range shows that whether a lattice cants is fixed by the local coordination geometry, whereas the value of the canting angle is set by the long-range tail. The quantum corrections are governed by the magnon spectrum through the size of the magnetic basis and are uncorrelated with frustration, so that the frustration and fluctuation classifications are independent; the corner-sharing lattices host the narrowest low-energy branches. The results constitute a spectral atlas of dipolar Heisenberg systems on the full family of $1$-uniform planar tilings and their duals, and provide predictions for inelastic neutron scattering in materials whose magnetic ions occupy Archimedean or Laves lattices, and for artificial arrays of dipolar-coupled nanomagnets.

</details>


### [153] [Dipolar order across Bravais lattice space: classification, spin waves, and a four-attractor phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02936)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在统一框架下研究了十四种Bravais格子在偶极相互作用下的经典基态与线性自旋波谱，识别出两个结构原理、一个Luttinger-Tisza构造失效的特例（面心正交晶格），并将Bravais空间坍缩到四个吸引子，其中体心四方为全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每种晶体都是Bravais格点加基矢构成，因此研究三维偶极磁性的系统理论必须以十四种Bravais格子的偶极有序为自然起点，但此前缺乏统一框架下的完整确定。

Method: 统一框架：对相互作用张量进行Ewald求和；通过在整个布里渊区最小化最低Luttinger-Tisza能带获得经典基态；对每个具有共线序的格子计算线性自旋波谱并加入零点修正。对每个格子族优化自由度量参数。

Result: 得出三项普适结果：(1) 偶极张量的精确无迹性与立方对称格子k=0处恒为零两个结构原理解释了有序类型、无一阶立方各向异性及零点矩减小的系统规律；(2) 面心正交格子是唯一击败Luttinger-Tisza构造的格子，其最优本征矢量非圆、单k态为长度不恒定的自旋密度波，表格能量为严格下界；(3) 优化自由参数后整个Bravais空间坍缩为仅四个吸引子，体心四方为全局最优，三角晶系α=62.42°处存在低于面心立方的单一内点最优。复现了三个独立已发表基准。

Conclusion: 该文提供了十四种Bravais格子偶极有序的系统性完整图景，揭示了超越单个格子的全局结构规律，并指出了标准Luttinger-Tisza方法的一个严格失效案例。

Abstract: Every crystal is a Bravais lattice decorated by a basis, so the dipolar ordering of the fourteen Bravais lattices is the natural starting point for any systematic theory of dipolar magnetism in three dimensions. We determine it here in a single common framework: the interaction tensor is Ewald-summed, the classical ground state is obtained by minimising the lowest Luttinger--Tisza band over the entire Brillouin zone, and the linear spin-wave spectrum with its zero-point corrections is computed for every lattice whose order is collinear. Three results emerge that are not properties of individual lattices but of the landscape. First, two structural principles --- the exact tracelessness of the dipolar tensor in three dimensions, and its identical vanishing at $\mathbf k=0$ for every cubic-symmetric lattice --- explain the ordering type, the absence of first-order cubic anisotropy, and the systematics of the zero-point moment reduction. Second, exactly one lattice defeats the Luttinger--Tisza construction: for face-centred orthorhombic the optimal eigenvector is not circular, the single-$\mathbf k$ state is a spin-density wave of non-constant length, and the tabulated energy is a strict lower bound; direct supercell minimisation gives the true ground state. Third, optimising each family over its free metric parameters collapses the whole of Bravais space onto only \emph{four} attractors, with body-centred tetragonal the global optimum and a single interior optimum at rhombohedral $α=62.42^\circ$ lying below face-centred cubic. Three independent published benchmarks are reproduced.

</details>


### [154] [Constituent-Tagged Transfer Linking Cluster Dynamics to Static Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02960)
*Kenji Shinoda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现团簇合并与分裂系统没有唯一的谱系（genealogy）。通过追踪守恒组分的主持尺寸（host size），定义了满足<S_{t+τ}/S_t>^(q*)=1的非零指数q*。在耦合映射网络、凝聚-碎裂动力学和Vicsek系统中，q*≈α-2（无需拟合），其中c(s)∝s^(-α)在同一尺寸窗口成立。半径扫描可定位已知的Vicsek相互作用半径，并恢复实验活性棒（active rods）中独立设定的团簇尺度。局部尺度协方差导出了这一关系，而tent map反例说明了当将每个母体尺寸处的稳态平衡简化为一阶矩时会失败的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团簇合并（coagulation）与分裂（fragmentation）过程普遍存在于多种物理、生物和社会系统中，但此类系统不存在唯一的谱系轨迹，导致难以用传统方式追踪团簇的历史演变。作者旨在提出一个不依赖明确谱系的通用统计量q*，用以刻画和预测此类系统的标度行为。

Method: 作者引入q*作为守恒量的矩指数，通过求解<S_{t+τ}/S_t>^(q*)=1来定义。在多种系统（耦合映射网络、凝聚-碎裂动力学、Vicsek活性粒子模型）中进行数值检验，将q*与团簇尺寸分布的幂律指数α对比，得到q*≈α-2的经验关系。最后通过局部尺度协方差推导理论解释，并用tent map构造反例检验方法适用边界。

Result: 在三种不同类型的系统中，q*均与α-2吻合良好且无需拟合参数。该方法不仅能从局部半径扫描中准确定位Vicsek相互作用半径，还能在实验活性棒体系中恢复独立设定的团簇标度。理论推导显示q*=α-2源于局部尺度协方差关系。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种无需明确谱系的通用统计量q*，揭示了合并-分裂动力学中一个普适的标度关系q*≈α-2。该方法可用于系统探测和标度估计，但tent map反例表明其成立需满足每个母体尺寸处稳态平衡可简化为一阶矩的条件，即局部尺度协方差的充分性假设。

Abstract: Clusters that merge and split have no unique genealogy. Tracking the host size of a conserved constituent defines $q_*$ by $\langle(S_{t+τ}/S_t)^{q_*}\rangle=1$, with $q_*\ne0$. Across coupled map networks, coagulation-fragmentation dynamics, and Vicsek systems, $q_*\simeqα-2$ without fitting, where $c(s)\propto s^{-α}$ on the same size window. Radius scans locate the known Vicsek interaction radius and recover an independently specified clustering scale in experimental active rods. Local scale covariance yields the relation, while a tent map counterexample shows when reducing the stationary balance at each parent size to one moment fails.

</details>


### [155] [Large deviations for linear regressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03111)
*Silvia Bartolucci,Fabio Caccioli,Francesco Caravelli,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用零温复本方法，在高斯设计与噪声、一般分布教师系数及可分正则化器（含Ridge与Lasso）设定下，解析计算了大规模线性回归最小训练损失的大偏差统计（率函数），刻画了最优损失的罕见样本间波动，并通过数值模拟验证了理论与尾部分布偏离高斯典型的显著行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性回归是最广泛使用的学习方法之一，其最优训练损失的统计特性对于理解学习动力学与泛化行为至关重要。传统分析仅关注典型波动（高斯行为），而罕见样本间的极端波动（大偏差尾部分布）尚未被系统刻画，尤其对于一般教师分布与一般可分正则化器。

Method: 采用零温复本方法（zero-temperature replica method），在P个预测变量与N个观测（r=P/N固定）、高斯设计与高斯噪声条件下，考虑教师系数独立采样自一般分布p(β)、损失为可分正则化器（含Ridge与Lasso），解析计算最小训练损失大偏差性质的率函数。理论与数值模拟进行对照。

Result: 得到了大样本极限下最小训练损失大偏差统计的解析率函数，该率函数控制最优损失的罕见样本间波动。广泛的数值模拟与理论高度吻合，并在尾部清晰地显示出相对于典型波动高斯回归的显著偏离。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了正则化线性回归最优训练损失在典型波动之外存在显著非高斯的罕见大偏差行为，为深入理解线性回归在高维极限下的极端样本行为提供了精确的理论框架，并可推广至更一般的正则化与数据分布场景。

Abstract: Linear regression is one of the simplest and most widely used tools to learn patterns from data: it fits a set of coefficients so that a linear combination of predictors best matches observed responses. The quality of the fit is measured by the residual sum of squares, the total squared mismatch between predictions and data, whose minimum defines the training loss. We consider Gaussian design and noise, with teacher coefficients independently drawn from a general distribution $p(β)$, and a general class of separable regularizers, including Ridge and Lasso. Using the zero-temperature replica method, we compute analytically the large-deviation statistics of the minimum training loss for large numbers $P$ of predictors and $N$ of observations, with $r=P/N$ fixed. The rate function we compute governs rare sample-to-sample fluctuations of the optimal loss. Extensive numerical simulations are in excellent agreement with our theory and clearly show a pronounced deviation from the Gaussian regime of typical fluctuations in the tails.

</details>


### [156] [Death by mutants: unusual multicritical dynamics in a two-species model for absorbing state transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03123)
*Astik Haldar,Abhik Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一个双物种（物种A及其突变体B）非互惠耦合模型中的活性-吸收态相变（AAPT），确定了一个连接全局吸收态与均匀活性态的多临界点。研究发现由于A和B之间的不对称耦合，两者具有不同的上临界维度（d_c^A和d_c^B）。在d<d_c^A时，多临界点表现出尺度不变性被波动诱导的对数调制所破坏的异常行为；而在d>d_c^A时恢复常规尺度不变性。突变体B的动力学属于DP普适类，其上临界维度为d_c^B=4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不对称（非互惠）耦合的双物种系统中的相变行为，理解突变体与原始物种之间不对称相互作用如何影响临界现象和普适性。

Method: 采用最低阶微扰理论研究多临界点处的临界动力学，分析两个物种各自不同的上临界维度以及波动诱导的对数调制对尺度不变性的影响。

Result: 发现物种A在d<d_c^A维度下出现尺度不变性被对数调制破坏的异常行为（order parameter和关联长度呈幂律行为），在d>d_c^A时恢复常规尺度不变性；突变体B属于DP普适类，上临界维度为4。

Conclusion: 不对称耦合导致两个物种具有不同的临界维度，并在多临界点上产生独特的临界行为，其中物种A表现出对数调制导致的尺度不变性破坏，而突变体B遵循标准的DP普适类行为。

Abstract: We explore the phases and active-to-absorbing state phase transitions (AAPT) in a two-species model, where the species A and its mutant B are {\em asymmetrically} or {\em nonreciprocally} coupled. We identify a multicritical point that connects the global absorbing state of both the species and its mutant, with a uniform active state. The critical dynamics at this multicritical point is studied within the lowest order perturbation theory. This asymmetric coupling between species A and mutant B leads to unequal and distinct upper critical dimensions $d_c^A$ and $d_c^B$ respectively for A and B dynamics. We show that the multicritical point in this model is characterised by an unusual breakdown of scale-invariance by fluctuation-induced logarithmic modulations with power law behaviour of the order parameter and correlation lengths of the species A at all dimensions $d<d_c^A$. Above $d_c^A$, conventional scale-invariance is restored. The dynamics of the mutant species B belongs to the DP universality class with an upper critical dimension of $d_c^B=4$

</details>


### [157] [Generative Nested Sampling of Atomistic Thermodynamic Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03193)
*Alessandro Coretti,Nico Unglert,Sebastian Falkner,Georg K. H. Madsen,Christoph Dellago*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-Flows算法，将基于条件归一化流的嵌套采样（NS）从引力波推断拓展到原子系统模拟。该算法利用条件流模型替代马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）更新，通过滑动窗口在实时活点集合上自洽训练，在Lennard-Jones盘系统中将能量评估次数降低两个数量级以上，并揭示了流动生成效率可作为物理诊断工具的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套采样（NS）能从单次模拟中解出原子系统的热力学性质，但其实际应用受限于马尔可夫链更新——需要在每个似然约束系综内使walker去相关。虽然基于流的NS已成功去除引力波推断中的这一瓶颈，但将其迁移到原子系统并非简单的应用转换，因为两者的似然景观存在根本差异，需要针对原子系统特性设计专门的流动模型方案。

Method: 首先对比GW150914型双黑洞似然与八粒子二维Lennard-Jones系统（维度相当），揭示原子系统多模态是离散且组合的（由粒子置换和硬碰撞壁产生），坐标耦合密集且集体性，而GW后验则具备平滑简并与局域参数耦合。基于这一诊断，提出NS-Flows：单一条件归一化流模型，以NS能量上界为条件，在最近活点集合的滑动窗口上训练，用直接并行抽样配合重要性加权拒绝重采样（IWR）替代MCMC。活点集合自洽提供训练数据，无需结构化先验或预制数据集。

Result: 在周期边界条件下的LJ磁盘系统中，NS-Flows将能量评估次数降低两个数量级以上，墙钟时间缩减约三分之一，且优势随势能成本增长而愈加显著。流的生成效率作为物理诊断量呈非单调变化，在稠密无序区域最低，可被约束系综的内部模式复杂度与训练窗口上的目标漂移定量刻画，表明液体状系综（而非先验-目标分离）才是当前流架构的难点。

Conclusion: 原子系统与引力波推断的似然景观存在本质差异，NS-Flows通过条件流加重要性加权拒绝重采样有效解决了原子系统的NS瓶颈，同时流的生成效率本身成为一种揭示系综物理特征的诊断工具，为未来流架构改进指明了方向——应着重处理液体状（密集无序）系综。

Abstract: Nested sampling (NS) resolves the thermodynamics of an atomistic system from a single simulation, but its practical reach is limited by the Markov-chain updates needed to decorrelate walkers within each likelihood-constrained ensemble. Flow-based NS has removed this bottleneck for gravitational-wave (GW) inference, yet its transfer to atomistic systems is not merely a change of application. Comparing a GW150914-like binary-black-hole likelihood with an eight-particle two-dimensional Lennard-Jones (LJ) system of comparable dimensionality, we show that the two landscapes differ fundamentally: atomistic multimodality is discrete and combinatorial, generated by particle permutations separated by hard collision walls, and its coordinate coupling is dense and collective, whereas the GW posterior exhibits smooth degeneracies and localized parameter coupling. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce NS-Flows: a single conditional normalizing flow, conditioned on the NS energy bound and trained on a sliding window of recent live sets, that replaces MCMC by direct parallel draws corrected by importance-weighted rejection resampling. Live sets supply data self-consistently, allowing flow training without structured priors or a pre-existing dataset. For LJ disks in PBC, the algorithm reduces energy evaluations by over two orders of magnitude and wall-clock time by roughly one third, an advantage that becomes increasingly favorable as the cost of the potential grows. The flow's generation efficiency further acts as a physical diagnostic: it varies non-monotonically along the annealing trajectory, is lowest in the dense disordered regime, and is quantitatively captured by the constrained ensemble's internal mode complexity together with target drift across the training window, identifying liquid-like ensembles, rather than prior-target separation, as the hard case for current flow architectures.

</details>


### [158] [Accelerating stochastic processes through nonequilibrium driving: Thermodynamic constraints on the maximum speed-up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03179)
*Davide Santolin,Gianmaria Falasco*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了一组新的界，用于控制微扰引起的轨迹依赖可观测量存活率加速的上限。通过研究两种不同背景下（连续时间马尔可夫跳跃过程和过阻尼扩散）的存活概率，得到了可实现加速增强的不同形式的界，这些界通过约束路径概率的非线性响应理论获得，并与热力学明确关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解微扰对瞬态过程（如稀有事件、存活概率）加速效应的理论极限，将动力学加速与热力学代价联系起来，为估计扰动可以多大程度地加速或减速轨迹依赖的可观测量的演化提供理论基础。

Method: 采用约束路径概率的非线性响应理论，推导存活率的加速界。针对任意时变存活率，得到与扰动强度和扰前熵产生率均相关的线性界；针对恒定存活率的稀有过程，得到由微扰产生的过量热决定的指数界。并在单环网络和双稳势阱中的受迫扩散两个原型系统上验证。

Result: 得到了两类不同形式的加速界：对任意时变存活率，获得结合扰动强度和背景熵产生率的线性界；对恒定存活率的稀有过程，获得由过量热决定的指数界。两种模型系统的数值示例验证了这些不等式的有效性。

Conclusion: 轨迹依赖可观测量存活率的加速受到由熵产生和过量热共同控制的基本热力学约束，线性界适用于一般时变速率情形，指数界适用于稀有过程，二者为理解非平衡微扰的动力学影响提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We derive a new set of bounds controlling the speed-up of the survival rate of trajectory-dependent observables induced by the application of a perturbation. Via the study of survival probabilities in two different contexts, continuous-time Markov jump processes and overdamped diffusions, we obtain different fashions of the bound on the achievable boost. These bounds, obtained by a nonlinear response theory of constrained path probabilities, are explicitly linked to thermodynamics. For arbitrary time-dependent survival rates, we find a linear bound that depends on both the perturbation strength and the entropy production rate prior to the perturbation. For rare processes characterized by a constant survival rate, the bound is exponential and is determined by the excess heat generated by the perturbation. We exemplify the inequalities on two prototypical model systems, namely, a unicyclic network and forced diffusion in a bistable potential.

</details>


### [159] [Optimal Control of Periodic Nonequilibrium Mechanochemical Systems via Automatic Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03217)
*W. Callum Wareham,David A. Sivak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用Fokker-Planck仿真的自动微分技术，为周期性分子机器设计高效的非平衡控制策略，涵盖有/无化学状态显式变化的模型，并揭示其高效驱动的设计原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子机器是在过阻尼、强涨落环境下周期性工作的介观系统。最优控制理论可用于理解其高效运行的设计原理，但以往对经典介观系统的最优控制研究大多局限于非周期性重复的控制问题，缺乏对周期性分子机器的系统性最优控制分析。

Method: 通过对Fokker-Planck仿真进行自动微分，为简单模型（含/不含化学状态显式变化）的周期性分子机器设计高效非平衡控制协议，并结合理论分析揭示驱动策略的设计原理。

Result: 设计出的控制协议和理论分析提供了关于此类非平衡系统中高效驱动设计原理的洞见：高效控制应通过以恒定角速度整体旋转角概率分布而不改变其形状来减少机械热；通过降低大热化学跃迁的比例来减少化学热。

Conclusion: 周期性分子机器的高效驱动应同时考虑机械与化学两个产热来源：机械方面采用刚性旋转概率分布的策略，化学方面抑制高耗散跃迁，为理解和优化生物分子机器的能效设计提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Biological molecular machines are mesoscopic systems that act repeatedly and periodically to perform important cellular tasks while contending with strong fluctuations and operating in an overdamped regime. Optimal control theory is a tool that can be used to understand the design principles behind efficient operation of these machines; however, most studies on optimal control of classical mesoscopic systems have focused on control problems that do not repeat periodically. Here, we automatically differentiate Fokker-Planck simulations to design efficient nonequilibrium control strategies for simple models of periodic molecular machines with and without explicit changes in the machine's chemical state. The designed protocols and theoretical analysis provide insight into the design principles governing efficient driving in these nonequilibrium systems. Designed control protocols should seek to reduce mechanical heat by rotating the entire angular probability distribution at a constant speed without changing its shape, and should reduce chemical heat by reducing the proportion of chemical transitions with large heat.

</details>


### [160] [Frenkel line of Yukawa fluids within the self-consistent relaxation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03606)
*Ilnaz I. Fairushin,Anatolii V. Mokshin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文基于自洽弛豫理论，在Yukawa流体中定义了动态交叉条件（Frenkel线），该条件与纵向类声学激发的色散关系中roton最小值的出现有关，并可从静态结构因子直接确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在简单流体（Yukawa流体）中建立Frenkel线的明确判据，并探索其与系统结构特征之间的内在联系，以便无需昂贵分子动力学模拟即可预测动态交叉位置。

Method: 采用自洽弛豫理论（self-consistent relaxation theory）分析Yukawa流体的纵向类声学激发色散关系，通过确定roton最小值消失的热力学状态来定位Frenkel线，并利用静态结构因子作为结构判据进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到的Frenkel线状态参数值与分子动力学模拟结果一致；证实Frenkel线可仅通过静态结构因子这一结构特征直接确定；并对Frenkel线附近roton最小频率给出了物理诠释。

Conclusion: Yukawa流体中Frenkel线的位置可通过解析理论结合结构因子直接预测，为简单流体的振动-扩散动态交叉提供了基于结构特征的普适判定方法。

Abstract: For a simple model fluid, the Yukawa fluid, the condition for dynamic crossover, known as the Frenkel line, is defined. The condition is related to the fact that the roton minimum in the dispersion relation of longitudinal acoustic-like excitations exists only when the collective vibrational dynamics of particles dominates in the liquid. Based on the self-consistent relaxation theory for the Yukawa fluid, thermodynamic states are determined in which the roton minimum disappears. The obtained values of the state parameters for the Frenkel line are consistent with the results of studies in which the position of this line on the phase diagram of the Yukawa fluid was determined using molecular dynamics simulations. It is shown that the Frenkel line in this system can be determined directly from the structural characteristic -- the static structure factor. A physical interpretation of the roton minimum frequency for the simple liquids near the Frenkel line is proposed.

</details>


### [161] [Sample-specific rectification-like response in a boundary-driven exclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03560)
*Issei Sakai,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究边界驱动的淬火位点无序对称排斥过程（SEP），通过Galerkin投影和平均场闭合近似分析稳态密度轮廓与电流响应，发现无序导致电流响应系数依赖于平均储库密度，并在个体无序实现中产生类似整流的行为，但系综平均后电流仍保持反对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序系统中边界驱动的输运行为，特别是非均匀位点速率对稳态电流响应的影响，以及无序造成的空间异质性如何产生样。

体特定的整流效应——这种效应并非来自外在的方向不对称，而是源于样本自身的空间异质性。

Method: 采用Galerkin投影在一阶线性层次近似稳态密度轮廓，在高阶使用平均场闭合。由此将电流表示为储库密度差的非线性函数，并分别考察线性响应系数、高阶偶数阶电流贡献，以及空间反射对称性对整流行为的约束。

Result: (1) 在线性阶，电流响应系数依赖平均储库密度ρ，异于均匀情况，并通过线性响应关系导致平衡电流涨落系数在ρ→1-ρ下不对称；(2) 线性响应之外，非零偶数阶电流贡献破坏电流在储库密度差反转下的反对称性，在单个无序实现中产生整流类行为；(3) 平衡密度轮廓的空间反射对称性可排除该类行为，故轮廓的空间反射对称破缺是整流类行为的必要条件；(4) 连续分布位点无序下，整流类行为几乎对任意无序实现在任意接近平衡处都存在；(5) 但系综层面由于无序系综的反射不变性，无序平均电流仍保持反对称。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了一种由样本特异空间异质性而非显式方向不对称驱动的整流类输运机制，为理解无序开放系统中非平衡输运提供了理论框架，并阐明了单样本与系综层面输运行为之间的重要区别。

Abstract: We investigate the current response of a boundary-driven symmetric exclusion process with quenched site disorder. Hard-core particles hop symmetrically on a one-dimensional lattice with site-dependent rates and are injected and removed at the boundaries by two reservoirs of different densities. We approximate the steady-state density profile using a Galerkin projection at linear order and a mean-field closure at higher orders, and thereby obtain the current as a nonlinear function of the reservoir density difference. At linear order in the reservoir density difference, the current-response coefficient depends on the mean reservoir density $ρ$, in contrast to the homogeneous case. Through the linear-response relation, this dependence leads to an equilibrium current-fluctuation coefficient that is asymmetric under $ρ\rightarrow 1-ρ$. Beyond linear response, nonzero even-order current contributions break the antisymmetry of the current under reversal of the reservoir density difference, producing rectification-like behavior in individual disorder realizations. We further show that spatial-reflection symmetry of the equilibrium density profile rules out such behavior, so broken spatial-reflection symmetry of the profile is a necessary condition for rectification-like behavior. Within the present approximation, we further find that, for continuously distributed site disorder, rectification-like behavior occurs arbitrarily close to equilibrium for almost every disorder realization. At the ensemble level, however, the disorder-averaged current remains antisymmetric because the disorder ensemble is invariant under spatial reflection. These results provide a mechanism for rectification-like transport arising from sample-specific spatial heterogeneity rather than from an explicitly imposed directional asymmetry.

</details>


### [162] [Equivalence classes of finite-time transitions in optimal control and non-equilibrium relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03862)
*Jan Meibohm,Samuel Monter,Clemens Bechinger,Sarah A. M. Loos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于结构化环境中随机系统最优控制的理论，揭示了这类控制问题普遍存在有限时间尖锐转捩（临界时间处控制策略的定性改变）。作者证明所有二次成本的最优控制问题可归为三类典型等价类（抛物型、双曲型、椭圆型），并为每类给出闭合形式的解。此外，作者建立了最优控制成本与大偏差速率函数之间的映射，并通过胶体粒子光镊实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机系统的有限时间动力学相变虽然已被人发现，但直接采样这类转变在数值上指数级昂贵。作者希望通过最优控制理论建立映射，使这类转变能通过普通平均（而非稀有事件采样）即可观测，从而为研究非平衡弛豫中的有限时间相变提供新的可行途径。

Method: 从过阻尼朗之万方程和二次型成本泛函出发，借助控制哈密顿量行列式的符号对问题进行分类（抛物、双曲、椭圆三种典型类），并为每类推导最优协议、成本函数和临界时间的闭式解。随后建立最优控制成本与位势淬熄后非平衡弛豫的大偏差速率函数之间的对应映射，并通过三个光镊胶体粒子实验（平均随机功的控制转变、自由扩散及谐振弛豫中的有限时间动力学相变）加以验证。

Result: 三类典型等价类的控制问题均可在临界时间处出现尖锐的有限时间转捩，且该转捩在平均场层面呈现连续相变特征；抛物型和双曲型类可映射到大偏差速率函数，椭圆型类则无简单弛豫对应。三种光镊实验均验证了理论预测的转捩现象。

Conclusion: 最优控制成本与大偏差速率函数之间的映射表明，有限时间动力学相变虽难以直接稀有事件采样，却可通过受控轨迹的普通平均值进行观测，为实验探测动力学相变提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We present a theory for the optimal control of stochastic systems in structured environments, represented by penalty terms in the cost functional. We show that such control problems generically feature sharp finite-time transitions associated with a qualitative change in the control strategy at a critical time. Starting from an overdamped Langevin equation and a quadratic cost functional, we show that all resulting problems fall into three canonical equivalence classes (parabolic, hyperbolic, and elliptic), distinguished by the sign of the determinant of the control Hamiltonian. For each class, we obtain the optimal protocol, the cost function, and the critical time in closed form, and show that the transition exhibits features of a continuous phase transition at mean-field level. We then establish a mapping between the optimal control cost and the large-deviation rate function governing non-equilibrium relaxation after a potential quench. The mapping covers the parabolic and hyperbolic classes, while the elliptic class has no simple relaxation counterpart. This correspondence implies that recently discovered finite-time dynamical phase transitions, which are exponentially costly to sample directly, are accessible through ordinary averages over optimally controlled trajectories. To validate our theoretical findings, we report three experiments with optically trapped colloidal particles: a control transition for the mean stochastic work, and the finite-time dynamical phase transitions in free diffusion and in harmonic relaxation.

</details>


### [163] [Effective Hamiltonian description on monitored Majorana chains: correlated power-law hoppings and unconventional entanglement scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04091)
*Ken Mochizuki,Hisanori Oshima,Ryusuke Hamazaki,Yohei Fuji*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过Lyapunov谱分析研究了单维马约拉纳链受监测动力学中有效哈密顿量的结构，发现其具有带非平凡幅度关联的随机长程幂律跳跃。文中构建匹配该特征的随机模型，证明长程跳跃幅度间的关联是导致纠缠熵呈现[ln(L)]²标度（而非常见的ln(L)标度）的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监测量子动力学中出现了无间隙相，其特征为有限尺寸标度不同于传统临界系统或无挫折系统：谱隙闭合速度快于1/L但慢于1/L²，纠缠熵增长为[ln(L)]²。理解这一非典型标度行为背后的有效哈密顿量结构，尤其是长程跳跃关联的作用，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者采用Lyapunov谱分析方法考察单维马约拉纳链受监测动力学的有效哈密顿量；构建随机长程幂律跳跃模型来捕捉有效哈密顿量的关键特征，并在有、无跳跃幅度关联两种情况下分别计算谱隙和基态纠缠熵的系统尺寸标度，进行对照分析。

Result: 发现有效哈密顿量具有随机长程幂律跳跃且幅度间存在非平凡关联（非独立同分布）；所构建模型的谱隙衰减快于1/L但慢于1/L²。无关联时基态纠缠呈ln(L)标度，有关联时纠缠熵增强且系统尺寸依赖与[ln(L)]²标度一致。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃幅度之间的关联是产生[ln(L)]²纠缠标度的根源，这类标度在传统孤立量子系统基态中极为罕见，该研究为理解受监测动力学中非常规纠缠行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the structures of effective Hamiltonians governing monitored dynamics of a one-dimensional Majorana chain through the Lyapunov spectral analysis. We focus on a gapless phase characterized by finite-size scalings different from those in conventional critical and/or frustration-free systems; the spectral gap closing faster than $1/L$ but slower than $1/L^2$ and the entanglement entropy growing as $[\ln(L)]^2$ with $L$ being the system size. We find that the corresponding effective Hamiltonians have random long-range power-law hoppings with nontrivial magnitude correlations, rather than being independently and identically distributed. To elucidate the role of these non-Gaussian correlations, we construct random power-law hopping models that capture the essential features of the effective Hamiltonians. The spectral gaps of the constructed models decay faster than $1/L$ but slower than $1/L^2$. We find that, in the absence of hopping correlations, the ground-state entanglement exhibits $\ln(L)$ scaling. In the presence of correlations, by contrast, the entanglement entropy is enhanced and its system-size dependence is consistent with $[\ln(L)]^2$ scaling over the system sizes studied. These results suggest that correlations among long-range hopping magnitudes are responsible for the entanglement scaling that seldom appears in ground states of conventional isolated quantum systems.

</details>


### [164] [Thermodynamic Concentration Inequalities: Controlling Uncertainty in Finite-Time and Small-Sample Thermodynamic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04162)
*Rick Bebon,Aljaž Godec*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了几何遍历扩散过程中广义电流及其样本均值偏离平稳均值的非渐近概率上界。该浓度测度行为由弛豫时间、局部耗散率和可观测量的内在局部涨落共同决定。研究发现系统在热力学平衡态与非平衡态之间存在显著差异，并提出了改进的逆热力学不确定性关系以及适用于小数据（短轨迹、小样本）的非渐近置信区间，首次定量回答了轨迹多长、样本多大才算"足够"的问题。作者以二维Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的电流观测为例，展示了该方法可用于严格检测细致平衡的破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学推断通常依赖渐近极限或大样本假设，但在实际应用中（如分子生物学中的单分子实验）只能获得短轨迹和小样本数据。目前缺乏针对广义电流在任意观测时长和任意样本量下的定量浓度界限，也不清楚何时数据足够大以支撑热力学推断，因此需要非渐近的浓度不等式来严格控制小数据场景下的推断不确定性。

Method: 作者针对几何遍历扩散过程的广义电流，推导非渐近浓度不等式（概率上界），其形式上由三大核心量控制：弛豫时间（刻画动力学收敛速度）、局部耗散率（刻画驱动强度）、以及可观测量的本地涨落。在此基础上推导了上界型的逆热力学不确定性关系，并据此构造出可用于任意小数据量的非渐近置信区间，以判定轨迹长度和样本量的充分性。

Result: 获得了广义电流偏离其平稳均值的非渐近上界，揭示了平衡态与非平衡态下波动行为的显著定性与定量差异；得到了将方差从上界约束的精细逆热力学不确定性关系；构建了短轨迹与小样本下热力学推断的非渐近置信区间；并以二维Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程在/离开平衡态的电流观测为例，验证了该方法可严格检测细致平衡的破缺。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为小数据下的热力学推断提供了严格的非渐近不确定性控制框架，既回答了"数据量多大才够"的基础定量问题，又为实验场景中利用有限轨迹严格检测平衡破缺提供了一种可行的方法论。

Abstract: We derive nonasymptotic upper bounds on the probability that a generalized current of a geometrically ergodic diffusion observed for any amount of time, or its sample mean over any arbitrary sample size, deviates from the stationary mean by more than any given amount. The concentration-of-measure behavior of generalized currents is universally governed by the relaxation time of the underlying dynamics, the locally observed dissipation rate, and the intrinsic local fluctuations of the observable. We uncover stark qualitative and quantitative differences in fluctuations in and out of thermodynamic equilibrium. We further obtain refined inverse thermodynamic uncertainty relations, bounding the variance of generalized currents from above. We construct nonasymptotic confidence intervals for controlling uncertainty in thermodynamic inference from small data, i.e., from short trajectories and small samples, and provide the first quantitative answer to when a trajectory is sufficiently long and a sample is sufficiently large. As an illustration, we apply our results to currents observed on a two-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process in and out of equilibrium and show how they can be used to rigorously detect broken detailed balance.

</details>
