### [165] [Standing waves for Schrodinger equations with Kato class potentials and $L^\infty$-bounded nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03098)
*Aleksander Ćwiszewski,Piotr Kokocki*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有Kato类势和L^∞有界非线性项的非线性Schrödinger方程驻波的存在性，涵盖非共振与共振情形。基于Conley指标理论分析相关抛物半流的Invariant集合，结合Feynman-Kac表示推导先验估计，进而获得驻波解与连接轨道的存在性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性Schrödinger方程的驻波存在性是非线性分析中的核心问题，特别是当势函数属于更一般的Kato类且非线性项具有有界Lipschitz常数时，经典变分方法可能失效，需要发展拓扑动力学方法。

Method: 采用Conley指标理论研究相关抛物半流的Invariant集合；利用Kato类势下Schrödinger半群的Feynman-Kac表示性质，推导L^∞和L^2范数下有界解的先验估计及Sobolev空间中的正则性界；通过分析驻定解之间的连接轨道获得驻波存在性。

Result: 在Lipschitz常数小于线性部分本质谱到零的距离条件下，分别在非共振和共振情形证明了驻波的存在性，并给出非平凡驻波解存在的充分条件。

Conclusion: 将Conley指标理论与Schrödinger半群的Feynman-Kac表示相结合，为具有Kato类势的一般非线性Schrödinger方程建立了一套统一的驻波存在性框架，扩展了此前对更特殊势函数类的结果。

Abstract: We establish the existence of standing waves for a nonlinear Schrodinger equation with potentials belonging to the Kato class and an $L^\infty$-bounded nonlinearity, whose Lipschitz constant is smaller than the distance from zero to the essential spectrum of the linear part. We consider both the nonresonant and resonant cases. Our approach is based on the Conley index theory applied to study invariant sets of the associated parabolic semiflow. Using properties of the Schrodinger semigroup with Kato-class potentials, which follow from its Feynman-Kac representation, we derive a priori estimates for bounded solutions in the $L^\infty$ and $L^2$ norms, as well as regularity bounds in Sobolev spaces. As a consequence, we obtain conditions ensuring the existence of connecting orbits between stationary solutions, which in turn yield the existence of nontrivial standing waves.

</details>


### [166] [Convergence rates to traveling waves for an FKPP model with nonlinear advection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03084)
*Ryan Patterson*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非线性平流项的反应扩散模型中行波解的收敛速率，通过引入形状缺陷函数并结合加权Nash不等式提出新的基于能量方法的途径，获得形状缺陷函数的L∞估计，进而确定收敛速率和波前位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具有非线性平流项的反应扩散模型，行波解的收敛速率问题此前尚未被充分研究。现有的收敛速率证明方法在处理非线性平流项时存在局限，需要发展新的技术手段来建立更精确的收敛估计，并同时确定波前位置。

Method: 本文采用形状缺陷函数作为核心分析工具来刻画行波解的偏差。提出了一种新颖的基于能量方法的技术路线，其关键在于引入加权Nash不等式，利用该不等式对形状缺陷函数建立L^∞范数估计，从而推导收敛速率。

Result: 成功建立了带非线性平流项的反应扩散模型中行波解的收敛速率，并同时确定了该模型的波前位置。

Conclusion: 通过将加权Nash不等式与能量方法相结合，本文为含非线性平流项的反应扩散行波系统的收敛性分析提供了新的有效工具，所获结果推广了该领域已有的收敛速率理论。

Abstract: We establish convergence rates of solutions to traveling waves for a reaction diffusion model with nonlinear advection. The convergence rate is proven using the shape defect function. We introduce a novel energy methods-based approach that uses a weighted Nash inequality to obtain $L^\infty$-estimates on the shape defect function. In determining the convergence rates, we also establish the front location for the model.

</details>


### [167] [The mean-field limit of the Schrödinger-Lohe model and emergent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03848)
*François Golse,Seung-Yeal Ha*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schrödinger-Lohe (SL)模型的严格平均场极限，给出了2-Wasserstein距离下一个边际分布与单粒子分布之间波动的定量估计，并为导出的动力学SL方程提供了导致完全同步和实际同步的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL模型是量子同步的非线性薛定谔方程组耦合系统，作为无限維Lohe矩阵模型的对应物，其严格的数学理论基础（如平均场极限和同步性分析）尚不完善，需要严格的数学分析来理解其动力学行为和同步机制。

Method: 通过严格的平均场极限论证来推导动力学SL方程，利用2-Wasserstein距离对单边际分布与单粒子分布之间的涨落进行量化估计，并基于所得方程分析同步的充分条件。

Result: 成功建立了SL模型的平均场极限，给出了涨落估计的定量界，并确定了保证动力学SL方程完全同步与实际同步的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该研究为SL模型的平均场极限提供了严格的数学证明，同时通过定量涨落估计和同步性充分条件的刻画，深化了对量子同步系统动力学行为的理解。

Abstract: The Schrödinger-Lohe (SL) model is a coupled system of nonlinear Schrödinger equations describing the temporal-spatial evolution of the component wave functions, and it corresponds to the infinite-dimensional counterpart of the Lohe matrix model for quantum synchronization. In this paper, we study a rigorous mean-field limit of the SL model and provide a quantitative estimate on the fluctuations between one-marginal distribution and one particle distribution in 2-Wasserstein distance. For the derived kinetic SL equation, we present sufficient conditions leading to the complete and practical synchronizations.

</details>


### [168] [The emergence of the Cauchy horizon from the crease for $3D$ compressible Euler flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03101)
*Leonardo Abbrescia,Jared Speck*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维可压缩欧拉方程在近平面对称数据下解的边界结构，证明柯西视界从"折痕"（crease）这一余维2类空子流形上产生，并与奇异边界共同构成数据的最大全局双曲发展（MGHD）边界的一部分。这是首次在无对称性假设的三维情形下描述O(1)尺度柯西视界的形成、结构和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解流体方程解的奇异性形成及其解的边界结构是双曲型偏微分方程领域的核心问题。此前的工作已证明梯度奇异性沿奇异边界产生，但柯西视界的形成及其结构性质尚不清楚。由于MGHD唯一性要求沿其整个边界验证特定结构性质，确定柯西视界及其性质对于保证经典解演化的唯一性至关重要。

Method: 作者采用动态适应折痕形状的新叶状化（foliation）时空构造，基于双零叶状化和先前导出的能量恒等式建立PDE框架。该框架旨在处理折痕缺乏严格凸性的新解区域，并克服动态熵与涡度拉伸（在二维等熵欧拉等简单情形中不存在）带来的困难。

Result: 证明了一个零超曲面——柯西视界——从折痕处生出，并沿垂直于奇异边界的方向传播。解在柯西视界上保持光滑，尽管感受到了折痕处梯度奇异性的影响。柯西视界、折痕和奇异边界共同构成数据MGHD边界的一个连通部分。作者验证了沿柯西视界和奇异边界的所需结构性质的局部版本。此工作提供了三维无对称假设下柯西视界O(1)尺度形成、结构与稳定性的首次描述。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一个关于三维可压缩欧拉方程奇异边界与柯西视界结构的全新数学框架，填补了在无对称条件下理解MGHD边界性质的空白，为未来证明MGHD唯一性及推广到更一般情形的可压缩欧拉系统奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: We study open sets of nearly plane symmetric data for the $3D$ compressible Euler equations with dynamic entropy and non-trivial vorticity. In our prior work, we proved that the solutions develop a gradient singularity along a singular boundary, which is a hypersurface that emanates from a co-dimension 2, spacelike submanifold called the crease. In the present paper, we prove that a null hypersurface, called a Cauchy horizon, emanates from the crease, and propagates in a direction transverse to the singular boundary. The solution remains smooth up to the Cauchy horizon, even though it ``feels'' the influence of the gradient singularity at the crease. The union of the Cauchy horizon, the crease, and the singular boundary, make up a connected portion of the boundary of a maximal globally hyperbolic development (MGHD) of the data. Roughly, an MGHD is the ``largest'' way that smooth data can evolve into a classical solution. Known examples show that the only way one can guarantee uniqueness of an MGHD is by proving that certain structural properties hold along its entire boundary. We prove that a local version of the needed properties are satisfied along the Cauchy horizon and singular boundary.
  Our work provides the first description of the formation, structure, and stability of an $O(1)$-size portion of the Cauchy horizon in $3D$ without symmetry assumptions. The presence of dynamic entropy and vorticity stretching, both of which are absent in simpler settings such as $2D$ isentropic Euler, introduces significant difficulties that we resolve with novel techniques. Our approach relies on new foliations of spacetime dynamically adapted to the shape of the crease, and a PDE framework based on double-null foliations and the energy identities we derived in earlier work. Collectively, our techniques allow us to handle a challenging new solution regime where the crease lacks strict convexity.

</details>


### [169] [Global existence and uniqueness of solutions in a chemotaxis system with density-suppressed motility, a $θ-$logistic source, and indirect signal production under a strong Allee effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03128)
*Om Tripathi,Sourav Kumar Sasmal,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含密度抑制游动性和带有强Allee效应的θ-逻辑斯蒂增长项的趋化-增长系统，在光滑有界域和齐次Neumann边界条件下，利用Schauder不动点定理、能量估计、L^p界、Gagliardo-Nirenberg插值不等式、半群估计及Moser迭代等技术，建立了该系统的全局有界古典解的存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 趋化-增长系统在生物学中具有重要应用，特别是描述细胞运动受信号浓度抑制的机制（密度抑制游动性），以及种群增长中强Allee效应的非线性影响。现有结果对这类耦合非线性系统的全局解存在性研究尚不充分，特别是当增长项和信号项具有高非线性（θ-逻辑斯蒂与w^β耦合）时，解的全局有界性问题尚未解决。

Method: 论文综合运用多种分析工具：（1）利用Schauder不动点定理证明局部时间经典解的存在性；（2）结合延拓准则、基本能量估计、L^p估计、Gagliardo-Nirenberg插值不等式、Young不等式、半群估计以及Moser型迭代格式来推导一致时间L^∞界，从而保证全局有界古典解的存在性。

Result: 当β<2(θ+2)/d时，系统保证存在唯一全局有界古典解。进一步地，若游动性的对数导数γ'(v)/γ(v)在[0,∞)上一致有界，且θ>(4-d)/(d-2)，则β的约束条件可放宽为β<[d(θ+1)+2(θ+2)]/(2d)。

Conclusion: 论文成功建立了带强Allee效应的密度抑制游动性趋化-增长系统的全局有界古典解的存在唯一性定理，并在额外的对数导数有界性假设下给出了更宽泛的参数容许范围，为该类非线性偏微分方程系统的理论分析提供了新的结果。

Abstract: We consider the following chemotaxis-growth system, featuring density-suppressed motility and a $θ$-logistic growth term that incorporates a strong Allee effect:
  \begin{equation*}
  \left\{
  \begin{aligned}
  u_t &= Δ(γ(v)u) + μu(1-u^θ)(u-A), & x \in Ω, \quad t > 0,
  v_t &= Δv - v+ w^β, & x \in Ω,\quad t > 0,
  w_t &= -δw +u, & x \in Ω, \quad t > 0,
  \end{aligned}
  \right.
  \end{equation*}
  subject to homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions in a bounded domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^d (d\geq 2)$ with smooth boundary. Here $μ\in \mathbb{R} , δ,β>0, θ\geq1$, and the positive motility function $γ(v) \in C^3([0,\infty))$ fulfills $γ'(v) \leq 0$ for all $v\geq 0$. The main objective of this paper is to establish the global existence and boundedness of classical solutions to the proposed problem. Our analysis combines the Schauder fixed point theorem for proving local-in-time existence with the extensibility criterion, fundamental energy estimates, $L^p-$bounds, the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality, Young's inequality, semigroup estimates, and a Moser-type iteration scheme to derive uniform-in-time $L^{\infty}-$bounds, thereby ensuring global bounded classical solutions. More precisely, we establish that the chemotaxis-growth system admits a unique globally bounded classical solution whenever $β< \frac{2(θ+2)}{d}$. Furthermore, if the logarithmic derivative of the motility function is assumed to be uniformly bounded on $[0,\infty)$, that is, $\frac{γ'(v)}{γ(v)} \in L^{\infty}([0,\infty))$, then the above restriction on $β$ can be weakened to $β< \frac{d(θ+1)+2(θ+2)}{2d}$, provided that $θ> \frac{4-d}{d-2}$.

</details>


### [170] [Existence of multi-peak solutions for singularly perturbed problems in the zero-mass case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03135)
*Léo Demuth*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在零质量情形下，奇异摄动椭圆问题多峰解的存在性，利用经典Lyapunov-Schmidt约化方法构造了围绕域内多个不同点集中且这些点为势函数非退化临界点的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究零质量情形下奇异摄动椭圆问题的多峰解存在性问题，将解的集中现象与势函数的临界点联系起来。

Method: 采用经典的Lyapunov-Schmidt约化方法，将无穷维问题约化为有限维问题来处理。

Result: 证明了多峰解的存在性，这些解在域内若干个不同内点附近集中，且这些集中点是势函数的非退化临界点。

Conclusion: 在零质量情形下，通过Lyapunov-Schmidt约化方法成功构造了奇异摄动椭圆问题围绕多个非退化临界点集中的多峰解。

Abstract: We prove the existence of multi-peak solutions for singularly perturbed elliptic problems in the so-called zero-mass case by applying the classical Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction method. The goal is to construct solutions concentrating around several distinct interior points of the domain, identified as nondegenerate critical points of a potential.

</details>


### [171] [Energy Decay Induced by Localized Coercivity in the 3D Navier--Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03164)
*Amadou Cissé*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对具有空间局部耗散的不可压三维Navier-Stokes方程，在动能和Leray-Hopf弱解层面建立了能量分析。引入连接黏性阻尼与全局L^2控制的局部强制性（coercivity）条件，并将其与Stokes算子的可观测性和谱不等式相联系。在该条件下，无需正则性或线性化假设即可建立指数能量衰减，并在非自治框架内处理含时阻尼。提出有限维谱量作为强制性的可计算代理，为降阶模型提供显式衰减估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维不可压Navier-Stokes方程的能量衰减行为在仅有空间局部耗散（而非全域均匀黏性）时，其动力学分析尤为困难。已有方法往往依赖正则性假设或线性化逼近，缺乏对弱解层面全局能量控制的系统性刻画。本文动机在于建立无需额外正则性假设的能量分析方法，并发展可计算的谱判据以量化衰减率。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 在Leray-Hopf弱解框架下进行动能水平分析；(2) 引入局部强制性条件，将其与Stokes算子相关的可观测性不等式和谱不等式建立联系；(3) 在强制性条件下证明无正则化假设的指数能量衰减；(4) 将问题拓展至含时阻尼的非自治框架；(5) 提出有限维谱量作为强制性的计算代理，用于降阶模型的显式衰减估计。

Result: 建立了局部耗散下三维Navier-Stokes方程的指数能量衰减结果，该结果在Leray-Hopf弱解层面有效且不依赖正则性或线性化假设；同时覆盖了非自治（含时阻尼）情形；提出的有限维谱代理量可为降阶模型提供显式且可计算的衰减率估计。

Conclusion: 本文为空间局部耗散的Navier-Stokes方程提供了严格且普适的能量衰减框架，将耗散的"局部→全局"控制能力归结为可验证的强制性/谱条件，为实际工程中的局部阻尼设计与降阶建模提供了理论支撑和量化工具。

Abstract: Energy-based analysis of the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations with spatially localized dissipation is developed at the level of kinetic energy and Leray--Hopf weak solutions. A localized coercivity property linking viscosity and damping to global $L^2$ control is introduced and related to observability and spectral inequalities for the associated Stokes operator. Under this condition, exponential energy decay is established without regularity or linearization assumptions, and time-dependent damping is treated within a nonautonomous framework. A finite-dimensional spectral quantity is proposed as a computable proxy for coercivity, yielding explicit decay estimates for reduced-order models.

</details>


### [172] [Exterior Serrin Rigidity for the Homogeneous{k}-Hessian Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03339)
*Zhihui Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了齐次k-Hessian方程在三维区域外的超定问题，在所有三种维度区域（通过参数q=(n-k)/k划分）中建立了球刚性结果。通过统一参数化和尺度函数F_q，结合接触点计算、边界粘性接触及在不同q范围采用不同的积分机制（Rellich-Pohozaev恒等式、Wronskian-Newton电流、Newton-Jacobi质量），证明了在边界满足|Du|=c>0且∂Ω严格(k-1)-凸时，Ω必为欧氏球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究k-Hessian方程外区域超定问题，旨在扩展经典Serrin型刚性结果到更一般的k-Hessian算子框架，涵盖全部三种维度区间（k<n/2, k=n/2, k>n/2），并统一处理三种不同边界条件和无穷远处增长条件的情形。

Method: 通过单参数q=(n-k)/k和尺度函数F_q统一地组织三种情形。关键步骤包括：1) 归一化变换U=F_q(v)使σ_k(M_q[v])=0成立；2) 统一的接触点计算得到尖锐上界|Dv|≤b；3) 边界粘性接触推导H_k≥qbH_{k-1}；4) 按q的临界值采用不同积分闭合机制：q>1用Rellich-Pohozaev恒等式，q=1用尺度不变的Wronskian-Newton流形，q<1用重整化的Newton-Jacobi质量；5) 对q≤1情况通过Minkowski规范构造全局严格k-凸定义函数，将构造从凸域推广到严格星形域。

Result: 在2≤k<n、Ω为光滑严格星形域的设定下，对三种维度区间分别建立球刚性结果：若|Du|=c>0在∂Ω上成立，则Ω必为欧氏球，且方程解可显式确定。此外对q≤1还得到了全局严格k-凸定义函数的构造。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了k-Hessian方程在所有三维区间上的外区域超定问题刚性刻画，通过统一的参数框架和分段的积分机制，建立了几何（球刚性）与解的结构之间的紧密联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the exterior overdetermined problems for the homogeneous k-Hessian equations $$σ_k(D^2u)=0\quad\text{in}~\mathbb{R}^n\setminus\overlineΩ$$ in three dimensional regimes. For $2\le k<n$ and smooth strictly star-shaped domain $Ω\Subset\R^n$, we establish ball rigidity results in all three dimensional regimes. If $2\leq k<\frac{n}{2}$, the solution with boundary value $-1$ and limit zero at infinity is treated under strict $(k-1)$-convexity of $\partialΩ$. If $k=\frac{n}{2}$, the solution with boundary value $0$ and logarithmic growth at infinity is considered under strict $(k-1)$-convexity of $\partialΩ$; if $k>\frac{n}{2}$, the solution with boundary $1$ and fundamental power-growth at infinity is considered under strict $(k-1)$-convexity of $\partialΩ$. In each case, for a positive constant $c$, $|Du|=c>0$ on $\partialΩ$ forces $Ω$ to be a Euclidean ball and determines the solution explicitly. The three arguments are organized by the single parameter $q=(n-k)/k$ and the scale function
  \[
  F_q(t)=\frac{t^{1-q}-1}{1-q},\qquad F_1(t)=\log t.
  \] After normalization, $U=F_q(v)$ and $M_q[v]=vD^2v-qDv\otimes Dv$ satisfies $σ_k(M_q[v])=0$. A unified contact-point calculation gives the sharp bound $|Dv|\le b$;a boundary viscosity contact then yields $H_k\ge qbH_{k-1}$. The integral mechanism that closes the argument changes at the critical exponent: Rellich--Pohozaev identities are used for $q>1$, a scale-invariant Wronskian--Newton current for $q=1$, and a renormalized Newton--Jacobi mass for $q<1$. When $q\leq 1,$ we also obtain a global strict k-convex defining function by Minkowski gauge, this help us extend the exterior construction from convex domains to strictly star-shaped domains.

</details>


### [173] [Twisted Gradient Flow Approach to Vegetation-Rainfall-Bushfire Interactions Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03386)
*Tahir Boudjeriou,Akio Ito*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个植被-降雨-林火相互作用模型，该模型由两个偏微分方程（描述林火强度和水分可利用性）和一个含非局部系数的常微分方程（控制植被密度）组成。作者将非局部系数视为底层实Hilbert空间度量结构变形的生成元，从而将ODE转化为状态依赖Hilbert空间上的扭曲梯度流，并建立了相关初边值问题强解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植被-降雨-林火系统是重要的生态动力学问题，其数学模型中的ODE含有一个非局部系数，使得数学分析极具挑战性。作者需要寻求新的视角来处理这个非局部项带来的困难。

Method: 将ODE中的非局部系数解释为实Hilbert空间度量结构变形的生成元（即度量张量的形变），据此将原始ODE重新表述为定义在状态依赖Hilbert空间上的扭曲梯度流（twisted gradient flow），并利用该几何框架分析初边值问题。

Result: 建立了所关联的初边值问题强解的存在性定理。

Conclusion: 通过引入度量变形的几何视角，成功绕过了非局部系数带来的分析困难，证明了系统强解的存在性，为该类生态模型的理论分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We consider a vegetation-rainfall-bushfire interaction model consisting of two partial differential equations describing the evolutions of bushfire intensity and water availability, together with an ordinary differential equation governing the vegetation density. The main feature of the model is that the ODE contains a nonlocal coefficient multiplying the vegetation density, which makes the mathematical analysis highly nontrivial. In this paper, we regard this nonlocal coefficient as the generator of a deformation of the metric structure of the underlying real Hilbert space. This interpretation enables us to formulate the ODE as a twisted gradient flow on state-dependent Hilbert spaces and to establish the existence of strong solutions to the associated initial-boundary value problem.

</details>


### [174] [The exterior Dirichlet problem for special Lagrangian equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03397)
*Yu Lei,Zhisu Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了特殊Lagrangian方程的外Dirichlet问题在指定无穷远处渐近行为下的存在唯一性定理，覆盖了所有相位的粘性解情形以及临界和超临界相位的经典解情形，并将内部Dirichlet问题推广到临界特殊Lagrangian方程。同时，作为应用，解决了三维二次Hessian方程的相应问题且无需任何可容许性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广此前第二位作者的工作，去除对渐近矩阵的限制性假设，并将衰减率改进到2-n阶，同时求解内部Dirichlet问题及相关三维二次Hessian方程问题。

Method: 在粘性意义下处理所有相位，在经典意义下处理临界和超临界相位；利用渐近行为与Dirichlet条件的结构进行分析，并推广至三维二次Hessian方程。

Result: 建立了外Dirichlet问题的存在唯一性定理，去除了对渐近矩阵的限制性假设并将衰减率改进至2-n阶；解决了临界特殊Lagrangian方程的内部Dirichlet问题；在无任何可容许性条件下解决了三维二次Hessian方程的对应问题。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推广了先前的结果，在更一般的条件下（包括无渐近矩阵限制、更优衰减率及免去可容许性条件）完善了特殊Lagrangian方程及三维二次Hessian方程的Dirichlet问题理论。

Abstract: We establish the existence and uniqueness theorem for the exterior Dirichlet problem for the special Lagrangian equation with prescribed asymptotic behavior at infinity, in both the viscosity setting for all the phases and classical setting for the critical and supercritical phases. These results generalize previous work by the second author by removing restrictive assumptions on the asymptotic matrix and improving the decay rate to the order $2-n$. We also solve the interior Dirichlet problem for the critical special Lagrangian equation and, as applications, all the above-mentioned corresponding problems for the three dimensional quadratic Hessian equation without any admissibility condition.

</details>


### [175] [Local well-posedness of the higher-order nonlinear Schrödinger equation on the half-line: the case of two boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03558)
*Aykut Alkın,Dionyssios Mantzavinos,Türker Özsarı*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了负色散系数的高阶非线性薛定谔方程在半直线、非零Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件下的局部Hadamard适定性。与正色散情形（只需一个边界条件）不同，该模型需要两个边界条件，这源于全局关系本身的结构性限制——最多只能消去一个未知边界迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究（Alkın, Mantzavinos, Ozsari, 2024）已建立了正色散情形的适定性，但负色散情形的边界条件需求及其适定性问题尚未被解决，且揭示了两者在全局关系结构上的本质差异。

Method: 利用Fokas统一变换推导受迫线性问题的显式解公式；建立半直线上的尖锐Sobolev与Strichartz估计；结合Cauchy问题的相应估计，证明高正则与低正则Sobolev空间中的局部适定性，并证明解映射的局部Lipschitz连续性。

Result: 在负色散系数情形下，零Dirichlet和Neumann边界数据下建立局部Hadamard适定性；发现了需要两个边界条件（而非正色散情形的一个）的关键特征，并在高低正则Sobolev空间中均获得局部适定性结果。

Conclusion: 负色散半直线问题与正色散情形在适定性框架上存在本质差异——需要两个边界条件——这一结构限制由全局关系决定；本文通过Fokas变换和线性估计完整地建立了该模型的局部适定性理论。

Abstract: We prove the local Hadamard well-posedness of the higher-order nonlinear Schrödinger equation with negative dispersion coefficient on the half-line under nonzero Dirichlet and Neumann boundary data. The paper uncovers a striking feature of this model: unlike the positive-dispersion case studied in (Alkın, Mantzavinos, Ozsari, 2024), well-posedness requires two boundary conditions rather than one. This reflects a structural limitation in the underlying global relation, which allows elimination of at most one unknown boundary trace. Using the unified transform of Fokas, we derive an explicit solution formula for the associated forced linear problem and establish sharp Sobolev and Strichartz estimates on the half-line. These linear estimates, together with corresponding Cauchy problem bounds, yield local well-posedness in both high- and low-regularity Sobolev spaces, as well as local Lipschitz continuity of the solution map.

</details>


### [176] [Extension Problems for the Vladimirov--Taibleson Operator and the Hierarchical Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03647)
*Yaojia Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了非阿基米德Caffarelli-Silvestre扩张理论，涵盖Vladimirov-Taibleson算子D^s与更一般的层级Laplacian算子L_C。作者在Bruhat-tits树与超度量树上配备适当的权重与边界条件，证明了Dirichlet问题的适定性、边界迹的收敛性及Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射与D^s一致，并建立了能量恒等式与显式的Fourier/Poisson表示，同时给出了p-adic AdS/CFT框架下的质量等价表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caffarelli-Silvestre扩张是欧氏空间中研究分数阶Laplacian的经典工具，但在非阿基米德（p-adic）情形下，将分数阶算子视为超度量调和测度理论中Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射的类似理论尚未建立。本文目标是在p-adic调和分析框架下发展完整的分部扩张理论，为p-adic AdS/CFT对应与层级Laplacians研究提供严格的分析基础。

Method: 作者在Bruhat-tits树T_{p^n}上施加沿边缘的权重w_{v_{k-1}v_k}=p^{k(s-n)}（基于horocyclic坐标），将原问题转化为带权重的调和Dirichlet问题。通过研究其端紧化上的连续解并利用边界迹与形变法向导数表征算子D^s，给出Fourier与Poisson积分表示及能量恒等式。随后将该构造推广到一般超度量空间上的层级Laplacian L_C：利用局部有限性与标度假设刻画典型边导纳（canonical edge conductances），要求消去Green算符（cancellation Green operator）在S_0(X)上与L_C^{-1}一致，从而得到唯一扩张与Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射等于L_C，并建立相应能量恒等式。此外还给出了无权重树上的质量等价表述（基于重整化迹与尺度修正法向导数）。

Result: 证明了带权的Dirichlet问题在端紧化上存在唯一的连续有界加权调和解；边界迹在一致拓扑与L^2意义下收敛到f，形变法向导数在逐点与L^2意义下收敛到D^s f；建立了加权树能量与D^s二次型之间的能量恒等式。对层级Laplacian L_C，在典型通量类中扩张唯一，Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射恰为L_C。Vladimirov-Taibleson构造作为齐次特例被恢复。

Conclusion: 本文为p-adic情形的分数阶Laplacian与层级Laplacians建立了一个完整的非阿基米德Caffarelli-Silvestre型扩张理论，其结构与经典欧氏情形高度对应（唯一性、边界迹收敛、Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射、能量恒等式齐备），并兼具p-adic AdS/CFT的物理动机，为该框架下的进一步调和分析与算子理论研究奠定了严格基础。

Abstract: We establish a non-Archimedean Caffarelli-Silvestre extension theory for the Vladimirov-Taibleson operator $D^s$ for $s>0$ and, more generally, for hierarchical Laplacians $L_C$. For every $f\in\mathcal{S}(\mathbb{Q}_p^n)$, we equip the Bruhat-tits tree $\mathcal{T}_{p^n}$ with the weight $w_{v_{k-1}v_k}=p^{k(s-n)}$ under the horocyclic coordinates. We prove that the resulting Dirichlet problem admits a unique bounded weighted-harmonic solution that is continuous on the end compactification. Its boundary traces converge to $f$ uniformly and in $L^2$, while the associated deformed normal derivative converges pointwise and in $L^2$ to $D^s f$. We derive explicit Fourier and Poisson representations of the extension and establish an energy identity between its weighted tree energy and the quadratic form of $D^s$. Motivated by $p$-adic AdS/CFT, we also give an equivalent massive formulation on the unweighted tree, based on a renormalized trace and a scale-corrected normal derivative. Finally, under natural local-finiteness and scaling assumptions, we extend the construction to hierarchical Laplacians $L_C$ on ultrametric spaces. We characterize the canonical edge conductances by requiring the cancellation Green operator on the ultrametric tree $\mathcal{T}_X$ to coincide with $L_C^{-1}$ on $\mathcal{S}_0(X)$. In the corresponding canonical flux class, the extension is unique, its Dirichlet-to-Neumann map is $L_C$, and it satisfies the associated energy identity. The Vladimirov-Taibleson construction is recovered as the homogeneous special case.

</details>


### [177] [A sharp mass threshold for boundedness in a critical quasilinear chemotaxis model with indirect signal production: The radially symmetric case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03713)
*Philippe Laurençot,Christoph Walker*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在径向对称条件下研究了高于二维空间中带有间接信号产生的临界拟线性趋化模型，利用系统的变分结构建立了尖锐的质量阈值 M_c > 0：质量低于 M_c 的所有解全局有界，而质量高于 M_c 且能量充分负的解全局存在但在无穷时间爆破。临界质量 M_c 的值与 Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev 不等式变体的最佳常数相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维空间（维数大于二）中带间接信号产生的临界拟线性趋化模型，探讨其解的全局存在性、有界性与爆破行为之间的临界质量阈值，揭示系统因变分结构而产生的尖锐质量临界现象。

Method: 利用系统的变分结构（variational structure），结合径向对称假设，将质量阈值问题与 Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev（HLS）不等式变体的最佳常数建立联系，从而精确刻画临界质量 M_c。

Result: 建立了尖锐的临界质量阈值 M_c > 0：当质量低于 M_c 时，所有解均为全局且有界；当质量高于 M_c 且能量足够负时，解全局存在但在无穷时间内发生爆破。同时确定了 M_c 与 HLS 型不等式变体最佳常数的精确关系。

Conclusion: 该模型在径向对称的高维空间中展现出与一维情况类似的临界质量行为，质量阈值由上临界（全局有界）和下临界（无穷时间爆破）两种行为分隔，且临界质量可精确由 HLS 不等式常数刻画，体现了变分结构对趋化模型全局动力学的主导作用。

Abstract: A critical quasilinear chemotaxis model with indirect signal production in space dimensions higher than two is studied under radial symmetry. Exploiting the variational structure of the system, the existence of a sharp mass threshold M\textsubscr{c} > 0 is established: all solutions with mass below M\textsubscr{c} are global and bounded, while those with mass above M\textsubscr{c} and sufficiently negative energy are global but blow up in infinite time. Moreover, the value of the critical mass M\textsubscr{c} is related to the best constant of a variant of a Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev inequality.

</details>


### [178] [Inverse Scattering for Single Photons in Quantum Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03726)
*Giovanni Covi,Matti Lassas,Medet Nursultanov,Lauri Oksanen,Mikko Salo,John C. Schotland*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究时谐单光子与静止两能级原子介质相互作用的逆问题。经时谐约化后，未知的紧支撑原子密度作为半拉普拉斯算子散射方程中的频率相关势出现。作者证明了三种强度数据类型的高频唯一性结果：源驱动测量、重正规化远场强度测量以及由相干平面波叠加得到的无相位远场测量。每种情况下，所有充分大频率下的对应数据均能唯一确定原子密度；源驱动结果需要源与观测集合满足几何可见性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从强度（而非相位）测量数据中唯一重建原子密度，解决量子光学中单光子散射的逆问题，此类无相位反散射问题在物理实验中更易获取但数学上更具挑战性。

Method: 将问题转化为半拉普拉斯算子的散射方程，将原子密度视为频率相关势；利用高频渐近分析技术和相干平面波叠加来从三种不同的强度测量数据中提取唯一性信息。对源驱动情形引入了几何可见性条件以保证数据充分性。

Result: 在源驱动的源/观测几何可见性条件下，以及重正规化远场强度和无相位远场测量中，所有充分大频率下的数据均可唯一确定原子密度。三种测量方案均获得高频唯一性结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为正则化与数值重构提供了理论唯一性基础，表明即使在无相位或仅强度测量的受限条件下，高频数据仍足以唯一重建量子光学系统中的原子密度分布。

Abstract: We study inverse problems for a time-harmonic one-photon model describing the interaction of a single photon with a medium of stationary two-level atoms. After time-harmonic reduction, the unknown compactly supported atomic density appears as a frequency-dependent potential in a scattering equation for the half Laplacian. We prove high-frequency uniqueness results for three types of intensity data: source-driven measurements, renormalized far-field intensity measurements, and phaseless far-field measurements obtained from coherent superpositions of incident plane waves. In each case, the corresponding data, given at all sufficiently large frequencies, determine the atomic density uniquely; the source-driven result requires a geometric visibility condition on the source and observation sets.

</details>


### [179] [Parabolic Poincaré inequalities and maximal function estimates for systems of partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03830)
*Wontae Kim,Juha Kinnunen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性偏微分方程系统解的抛物型Poincaré不等式，证明了这些不等式具有自改进性质，并基于逐点极大函数估计将其应用于反对数圆柱和梯度上的平均振荡的reverse Hölder不等式，同时探讨了固定空间点上时间区间的对应结果，最终获得时间正则性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛物型Poincaré不等式在非线性PDE系统的正则性分析中具有基础性作用。现有结果通常假设不等式成立，但缺乏对其自改进性质的研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，并通过自改进性质推导出一系列重要的推论，特别是针对抛物型问题中时间和空间方向的不对称性，需分别考察时间方向的Poincaré不等式与正则性。

Method: 本文的核心方法是建立逐点极大函数估计（pointwise maximal function estimate），利用该估计作为桥梁来证明抛物型Poincaré不等式的自改进性质。通过自改进的直接结果，进一步推导反对数圆柱上平均振荡的reverse Hölder不等式以及梯度的相应不等式。论文还针对固定空间点上的时间区间单独建立了Poincaré不等式及其自改进理论。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）非线性PDE系统解的抛物型Poincaré不等式具有自改进性质；（2）由此获得反对数圆柱上平均振荡和梯度的reverse Hölder不等式；（3）在固定空间点的时间区间上，相应的Poincaré不等式和自改进结果也成立；（4）作为应用，获得了解在时间方向上的正则性结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过逐点极大函数估计这一关键工具，系统建立了抛物型Poincaré不等式的自改进理论，并将其成功应用于获取非线性系统解的时间方向正则性。该工作为抛物型偏微分方程系统的正则性分析提供了统一的方法论框架。

Abstract: We study parabolic Poincaré inequalities for solutions to nonlinear systems of partial differential equations. Our main results show that these inequalities are self-improving. As applications, we establish reverse Hölder inequalities for the mean oscillation over parabolic cylinders and for the gradient. We also consider the corresponding Poincaré inequalities and self-improvement results on time intervals at fixed spatial points. These results are based on a pointwise maximal function estimate. As an application, we obtain regularity results in the time direction for solutions to nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [180] [On flows generated by square-integrable vector fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03831)
*Nikolay A. Gusev,Mikhail V. Korobkov,Evgeny Yu. Panov,Konstantin Yu. Zamana*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对于$\mathbb{R}^d$上的平方可积无散度向量场$\boldsymbol{v}$，以下三个性质等价：(1)算子$A_0\rho=\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla\rho$在$L^2$上本质斜自伴；(2)连续性方程的平方可积广义解是重整化的；(3)相应连续性方程Cauchy问题的平方可积广义解在时间前向和后向均唯一。此外，论文构造了一个$\mathbb{R}^3$上的紧支撑有界无散度向量场，使得相应的Cauchy问题解在时间前向唯一而后向不唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立无散度向量场所生成的对流算子的本质斜自伴性、连续性方程广义解的重整化性质以及Cauchy问题解的唯一性三者之间的深刻联系，从而从算子理论和PDE理论两个角度统一理解无散度流场中输运现象的数学本质。

Method: 论文通过严格的函数分析论证，建立了关于本质斜自伴性、重整化解性质和Cauchy问题解的时间前向与后向唯一性之间的等价性定理。同时，通过显式构造一个定义在$\mathbb{R}^3$上的紧支撑有界无散度向量场，展示了时间前向唯一性与后向唯一性可以分离（前向唯一但后向不唯一），从而揭示了该等价性框架下时间方向对称性的微妙破缺。

Result: 论文证明了上述三个性质的等价性，并构造了一个具体的$\mathbb{R}^3$上的紧支撑有界无散度向量场反例，该反例表明连续性方程的解可以是时间前向唯一但后向不唯一的，展示了对流算子理论中时间方向不对称的新现象。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了无散度向量场输运理论中算子理论性质、PDE解的正则性性质与唯一性性质之间的统一等价框架，并通过反例表明在一般（非Lipschitz）的无散度流场中，时间前向与后向唯一性并非总是同时成立，深化了对非光滑无散度流中输运方程理解。

Abstract: For square-integrable divergence-free vector field $\boldsymbol{v}$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$ we prove that the following properties are equivalent:
  1) the operator $A_0 ρ= \boldsymbol{v} \cdot \nabla ρ$ (where $ρ\in C^\infty_c(\mathbb{R}^d)$) is essentially skew-adjoint on $L^2(\mathbb{R}^d)$;
  2) square-integrable (with respect to spatial variables) generalized solutions of the continuity equation are renormalized;
  3) generalized square-integrable (with respect to spatial variables) solutions of the Cauchy problem for the corresponding continuity equation are unique both forward an backward in time.
  We also construct a compactly supported bounded divergence-free vector field $\boldsymbol{v}\colon \mathbb{R}^3 \to \mathbb{R}^3$ for which square-integrable (with respect to spatial variables) solutions of the Cauchy problem for the corresponding continuity equation are unique forward, but not backward in time.

</details>


### [181] [A counterexample to McKean's conjecture for the Landau-Coulomb equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03847)
*Jonathan Junné,Raphael Winter,Havva Yoldaş*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文否证了McKean猜想，该猜想认为空间均匀Landau-Coulomb方程的解中熵耗散单调不增（即熵关于时间是凸的）。作者通过构建一个由麦克斯韦平衡态加上径向对称、光滑、尺度R≫1的环状扰动构成的显式反例，证明了该猜想不成立。将熵耗散的导数按R的幂次展开后发现，恰当选择的扰动可使首阶项为正。该反例在相对熵和标准Sobolev范数下都接近平衡态，且由于Landau方程是Boltzmann方程在掠射碰撞极限下的近似，此反例也表明McKean猜想对一般Boltzmann方程不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McKean猜想是关于Landau-Coulomb方程熵耗散单调性的长期未解决问题，其真实性对理解该方程的松弛行为和时间收敛结构具有重要意义。作者旨在检验并否证这一长期假设。

Method: 构造显式反例：以麦克斯韦平衡态为基础，叠加一个位于半径尺度R≫1的环上的径向对称光滑扰动。通过对熵耗散的时间导数按R的幂次做渐近展开分析，考察首阶项的符号，并选取合适的扰动形式使首阶项为正，从而构造出违反熵耗散单调性的解。

Result: 成功构造出显式反例，其中熵耗散在某一时刻增加而非单调非增，否证了McKean猜想。反例虽在相对熵和Sobolev范数下靠近平衡态，但其渐近展开中的正首阶项确保了熵耗散的非单调性。

Conclusion: McKean猜想对Landau-Coulomb方程不成立；作为推论，该猜想对一般Boltzmann方程在掠射碰撞极限下也不能成立。这一结果修正了学界对这一经典动力学历程性问题的认知。

Abstract: We disprove McKean's conjecture, which asserts that the entropy dissipation is monotone nonincreasing along solutions, or equivalently that the entropy is convex in time, for the spatially homogeneous Landau-Coulomb equation. We provide an explicit counterexample which consists of a Maxwellian equilibrium under radially symmetric, smooth perturbation on an annulus of scale $R\gg 1$. Developing the derivative of the entropy dissipation in powers of $R$ reveals that the leading order terms can be positive for a suitably chosen perturbation. Remarkably, the counterexamples are close to equilibrium in relative entropy and standard Sobolev norms. Since the Landau equation arises from the Boltzmann equation in the grazing collisions limit, our counterexample also shows that McKean's conjecture is not true in general for the Boltzmann equation.

</details>


### [182] [Sharp asymptotics for a transport model with a nonlocal condition of the Fisher-KPP type at the boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03869)
*Grégory Faye,Jean-Michel Roquejoffre,Mingmin Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半平面中传输问题耦合非线性非局部边界条件的精确渐近行为。这类系统源于流行病空间传播模型（空间无关版本即经典Kermack-McKendrick模型）。利用Fisher-KPP型非局部方程的研究思想并借助模型特殊结构，证明了任何初始局域化解会滞后于最小行波，滞后量随时间对数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解流行病空间传播模型的长期渐近行为，特别是初始局域化解与最小行波之间的精确关系，这是解析流行病传播动态的重要理论问题。该问题的难点在于系统包含非线性非局部边界条件，经典的Fisher-KPP理论不能直接适用。

Method: 借鉴非局部Fisher-KPP型方程的研究思想，结合模型特有的结构（如Kermack-McKendrick模型的空间扩展形式），通过构造适当的上下解或精细的渐近分析来刻画解在时间趋于无穷时的行为。

Result: 证明了任何初始局域化的解在长时间下总是滞后于最小行波，且滞后延迟随时间的对数函数增长（即呈O(log t)量级）。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了流行病空间传播模型中解长期行为的精确渐近刻画，揭示了此系统与标准Fisher-KPP型方程之间的本质差异——解无法追上最小行波速度对应的前沿位置，而是以对数级延迟滞后。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the precise asymptotics, as time goes to infinity, of a transport problem in a half plane coupled with a nonlinear nonlocal boundary condition. This system arises from a class of models for the spatial spread of epdemics, its space independent version being the classical Kermack-McKendrick model. Using ideas pertaining to the study of nonlocal equations of the Fisher-KPP type, and exploiting the particular structure of the model, we prove that any initially localized solution will lag behind the minimal traveling wave, with a delay that grows logarithmically in time.

</details>


### [183] [A Critical Chemin--Lerner Regularity Criterion via One Velocity Component for the Three-Dimensional Navier--Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03877)
*Maotuo Guo,Wendong Wang,Shiyang Xiong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程有限能量适定弱解的一个临界正则性准则，仅依赖速度场的一个分量u^3。该准则要求u^3属于Besov空间L̃^p(0,T;Ḃ^0_{m,1}(R³))，即空间频率的ℓ^1细化（补充于Wang-Wu-Zhang的时间Lorentz细化），并通过Bernstein嵌入推广到非负光滑临界线。核心创新是嵌入局部能量不等式中的频率-尺度匹配方案，通过双端ℓ^1核将空间频率可和性转化为物理尺度能量增量的可和性，从而获得无需时间Lorentz细化的统一Type-I局部能量界，最终通过紧性及单分量刚性排除奇异blow-up极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维Navier-Stokes方程的正则性问题是数学物理中的核心开放问题。已有的临界条件u^3∈L^p_tL^m_x尚未解决，作者试图通过空间频率的ℓ^1细化来获得可证明的正则性准则，同时补充Wang、Wu和Zhang等人关于时间Lorentz细化的工作，朝解决这一开放问题迈出一步。

Method: 作者提出了一个频率-尺度匹配方案并嵌入局部能量不等式分析中：1) 保留u^3的每个dyadic块直到其与一维后向热核选择的垂直尺度配对；2) 低频部分利用板厚度收益，高频部分通过将投影转移到局部通量并应用逆Bernstein估计；3) 空间分离的压力源通过调和衰减处理。这些机制共同生成一个双端ℓ^1核，将空间频率可和性转化为物理尺度能量增量的可和性。此外结合Bernstein嵌入推广到非负光滑临界线s=-1+2/p+3/q≥0上的Besov空间。

Result: 建立了如下正则性准则：若∑_{j∈Z}‖Δ̇_j u^3‖_{L^p(0,T;L^m(R³))}<∞（其中2<p<∞，m=3p/(p-2)），即u^3∈L̃^p(0,T;Ḃ^0_{m,1}(R³))，则有限能量适定弱解不会出现奇点。该结果通过Bernstein嵌入推广至u^3∈L̃^p_tḂ^s_{q,1}（s=-1+2/p+3/q≥0），并获得统一的Type-I局部能量界。作为推论，紧性与单分量刚性排除了第三速度分量消失的奇异blow-up极限。

Conclusion: 该准则在临界条件下给出了仅依赖单一速度分量的严格正则性判据，作为空间频率ℓ^1细化版本，与Wang-Wu-Zhang的时间Lorentz细化结果互补。研究为最终解決单分量临界正则性问题提供了重要的方法论基础，其频率-尺度匹配技术可能对更广泛的流体动力学奇异分析具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: We prove a scaling-critical regularity criterion involving only one velocity component for finite-energy suitable weak solutions of the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. Let $2<p<\infty$ and $m=3p/(p-2)$, so that $2/p+3/m=1$. We show that a singularity cannot occur provided \[
  \sum_{j\in\mathbb Z}
  \|\dotΔ_j u^3\|_{L^p(0,T;L^m(\mathbb R^3))}<\infty, \] that is, $u^3\in\widetilde L^p(0,T;\dot B^0_{m,1}(\mathbb R^3))$. The assumption is a spatial-frequency $\ell^1$ refinement of the still unresolved critical condition $u^3\in L^p_tL^m_x$ and is complementary to the Lorentz-in-time refinement $L^{p,1}_tL^m_x$ obtained by Wang, Wu, and Zhang. By Bernstein embedding, the result extends to $u^3\in\widetilde L^p_t\dot B^s_{q,1}$ on the nonnegative-smoothness critical line $s=-1+2/p+3/q\ge0$.
  The principal innovation is a frequency--scale matching scheme embedded in the local energy inequality. Each dyadic block of $u^3$ is retained until it is paired with the vertical scale selected by a one-dimensional backward heat kernel. Low frequencies gain from the slab thickness, high frequencies from transferring the projection to the localized flux and applying an inverse Bernstein estimate, and spatially separated pressure sources from harmonic decay. These mechanisms generate a two-sided $\ell^1$ kernel, converting spatial-frequency summability into summability of physical-scale energy increments. Consequently, we obtain a uniform Type-I local energy bound without a Lorentz refinement in time; compactness and one-component rigidity then exclude singular blow-up limits whose third velocity component vanishes.

</details>


### [184] [Halfspace Theorems for Anisotropic Minimal Surfaces and Perimeter Minimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03916)
*Yang Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了各向异性均匀椭圆参数积分泛函的两个半空间定理：在\mathbb{R}^3中，对偶函数的连通光滑真嵌入无边界各向异性极小曲面若位于半空间中则必为平面；在任意维数且不假设对偶性的条件下，整体各向异性周长极小化泛函若其非空边界位于半空间中，则该泛函本身必为半空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Hoffman-Meeks半空间定理（针对极小曲面）推广到各向异性（非欧氏）情形。经典证明依赖悬链面（catenoid）作为外部图，但各向异性情形下悬链面不再适用，需要寻找新的几何工具；同时希望在不假设对偶性的一般各向异性情形下也获得相应的半空间刚性结果。

Method: 证明分两个部分：(1) 在\mathbb{R}^3中，用基于椭圆对数的严格外部图（strict exterior graph）替代经典Hoffman-Meeks论证中的悬链面；(2) 在一般维度中，将壁接触（wall contact）与平面剥离（plane-peeling）论证相结合，将任何残留的相缺陷转化为反向积分泛函（reversed integrand）的极小化子，并通过第二次blow-down将其排除。

Result: 建立了两个定理：(i) 在三维空间中，对于偶函数的各向异性均匀椭圆参数积分泛函，位于半空间中的连通光滑真嵌入无边界极小曲面必为平面；(ii) 在任意维数且无需对偶性假设下，边界位于半空间中的整体各向异性周长极小化子本身必为半空间（即其边界支撑一个超平面）。

Conclusion: 本文成功将经典极小曲面理论中的Hoffman-Meeks半空间定理推广到各向异性参数积分泛函框架，并通过引入新的几何与变分论证（椭圆对数外部图、壁接触与平面剥离）克服了各向异性情形下传统工具失效的困难，给出了两个不同层面的完备回答。

Abstract: We prove two anisotropic halfspace theorems for uniformly elliptic parametric integrands. In $\mathbb{R}^3$, every connected smooth properly embedded boundaryless anisotropic minimal surface for an even integrand is a plane if it lies in a halfspace. The proof replaces the catenoid in the Hoffman--Meeks argument by a strict exterior graph modeled on an elliptic logarithm. In arbitrary dimension and without assuming evenness, every global anisotropic-perimeter minimizer whose nonempty boundary lies in a halfspace is itself a halfspace. The proof combines wall contact with a plane-peeling argument that turns any residual phase defect into a minimizer for the reversed integrand and excludes it by a second blow-down.

</details>


### [185] [Interior Curvature Estimates of Semi-convex Solutions for the scalar curvature equation with Lipschitz Right-Hand Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03958)
*Lichun Liang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维容许图（2-admissible graph）在σ₂(κ[u])=f(x)方程下的内部曲率估计，证明了依赖于右端项Lipschitz范数的内部曲率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于满足σ₂(κ[u])=f(x)的2-可容许图方程，需要建立内部曲率估计，且此估计应依赖于右端项f的Lipschitz范数（而非更高的正则性要求），以推广已有结果并完善该领域的理论。

Method: 证明方法包含三个关键步骤：(1) 引入平移的Jacobi不等式用于b=log(H+J₀)；(2) 利用平行超曲面变换使Newton张量一致椭圆化；(3) 通过局部有界性估计将逐点界化为加权L¹估计，最终利用Jacobi能量不等式和分部积分完成证明。

Result: 成功获得了满足σ₂(κ[u])=f(x)的2-可容许图的内部曲率估计，该估计仅依赖于右端项的Lipschitz范数。

Conclusion: 该结果表明对于这类方程，曲率估计的依赖条件可以降至右端项的Lipschitz范数，为后续更一般的曲率方程研究提供了方法基础。

Abstract: In this paper, let $u\in C^4(B_{10})$ with $D^2u\geq -KI$ define a $2$-admissible graph $M=\{(x,u(x)):x\in B_{10}\}\subset\R^{n+1}$ satisfying \[ σ_2(κ[u])=f(x). \] We prove an interior curvature estimate depending on the Lipschitz norm of the right-hand sides. The proof combines a shifted Jacobi inequality for \(b=\log(H+J_0)\), a parallel hypersurface transformation that makes the Newton tensor uniformly elliptic and local boundedness estimate then reduces the pointwise bound to a weighted $L^1$ estimate, which is completed using the Jacobi energy inequality and integration by parts.

</details>


### [186] [$L^p$ isomorphisms for the fractional Laplacian on $\mathbb{R}^n$ and their application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03962)
*Congming Li,Yugao Ouyang,Zixuan Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在Amrouche-Girault-Giroire加权Sobolev空间经典框架的启发下，建立了分数阶Laplacian的L^p理论：引入以分数阶Laplacian的L^p范数作为最高阶度量的加权分数阶Sobolev空间Λ^{s,p}(R^n)，证明对所有s∈(0,2)、p∈(1,∞)，(−Δ)^{s/2}是从该空间模掉有限维多项式空间到L^p的同构；通过对偶延拓到负阶数，分数阶Laplacian在相应空间之间均互为同构且可精确复合。关键工具是适配该范数的加权Hardy和Poincaré不等式（通过约化到梯度证明），并借助整个s-调和函数的性质解决稠密性问题。作为应用，给出分数阶Stokes系统在R^n上带显式核与相容条件的适定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Laplace方程在全空间R^n上的加权Sobolev空间理论早已由Amrouche、Girault和Giroire（1994）解决，但分数阶Laplacian作为非局部算子，其相应的加权L^p理论尚属空白。本文旨在将经典框架推广到分数阶情形，建立fractional Laplacian在全空间上的同构定理，从而保证方程(−Δ)^{s/2}u=f对任意f∈L^p可解且映射结构完整，并以Stokes系统作为实际应用验证理论价值。

Method: (1) 引入加权分数阶Sobolev空间Λ^{s,p}(R^n)，以∥(−Δ)^{s/2}u∥_{L^p}作为最高阶范数度量；(2) 对1≤s<2借助约化到梯度的方法建立适配该半模的加权Hardy与Poincaré不等式；(3) 鉴于这些不等式仅先验地在稠密类上成立，以及C_c^∞稠密性本身是难点，先在Schwartz类的闭包上证明同构；利用两个解之差是整体s-调和函数从而必为多项式的事实，证明该闭包与Λ^{s,p}(R^n)一致，稠密性作为副产品得到；(4) 通过对偶将尺度延拓到负数阶，实现分数阶Laplacian在同构意义下的精确复合；(5) 将框架应用于分数阶Stokes系统，推导显式核与相容条件。

Result: 证明了对所有s∈(0,2)、p∈(1,∞)，映射(−Δ)^{s/2}:Λ^{s,p}(R^n)/P_{[s−n/p]}→L^p(R^n)是同构，故方程(−Δ)^{s/2}u=f对每个f∈L^p有唯一解（模掉显式的有限维多项式空间）。通过对偶延拓到负阶，建立了服从精确复合律的同构链。同时得到了分数阶Stokes系统在全空间上带显式核与相容条件的存在唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功将经典加权Sobolev空间理论系统性地推广到分数阶Laplacian的L^p框架，建立了一族完整且可精确复合的加权分数阶Sobolev空间同构理论，填补了该领域的空白，并展示了其在分数阶Stokes系统等应用中的有效性。

Abstract: A classical theory of Amrouche, Girault and Giroire (1994) resolves the Laplace equation on $\mathbb{R}^n$ through an isomorphism between weighted Sobolev spaces. Inspired by their framework, we develop the corresponding $L^p$ theory for the fractional Laplacian: we introduce weighted fractional Sobolev spaces $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$, gauged at top order by $\|(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u\|_{L^p}$, and prove that $(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}:Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathcal P_{[s-n/p]}\to L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is an isomorphism for all $s\in(0,2)$ and $p\in(1,\infty)$; thus $(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u=f$ is solvable for every $f\in L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, uniquely modulo an explicit finite-dimensional space of polynomials. More generally, extending the scale by duality to negative orders, the fractional Laplacians of the appropriate orders map its spaces isomorphically onto one another and compose exactly. A key ingredient is a family of weighted Hardy and Poincaré inequalities adapted to $\|(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u\|_{L^p}$, established here for $1\le s<2$ by a reduction to the gradient. Since these inequalities hold a priori only on a dense class, while the density of $C_{\mathrm{c}}^\infty(\mathbb{R}^n)$ in $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is itself nontrivial, we first prove the isomorphism on the closure of the Schwartz class, which coincides with $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ since the difference of two solutions is an entire $s$-harmonic function, hence a polynomial; the density follows as a by-product. As an application, we obtain existence and uniqueness, with explicit kernels and compatibility conditions, for the fractional Stokes system in $\mathbb{R}^n$.

</details>


### [187] [An exact traveling waves solution for a special class of semilinear equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03974)
*Kirill A. Dekhnich*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对具有特定非线性项的半线性抛物方程，构造了精确的行波解。该非线性项源于基于分支布朗运动的投票模型，且此前已知的结果均为本文结果的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性抛物方程的行波解在数学物理和应用领域（如投票模型）中具有重要理论意义，而此前仅得到部分特例解，缺乏更一般性的精确行波解构造。

Method: 针对由分支布朗运动投票模型导出的特定非线性形式，采用精确构造方法求解半线性抛物方程的行波解。

Result: 获得了在该特定非线性下方程的精确行波解族，并统一推广了此前已知的若干特例结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的精确行波解涵盖了此前所有已知情形作为特殊情形，为相关模型提供了更完整、更一般的数学刻画。

Abstract: We construct exact traveling-wave solutions for a semilinear parabolic equation with a specific nonlinearity. This nonlinearity arises, in particular, in voting models based on branching Brownian motion. Previously known results are special cases of those presented here.

</details>


### [188] [Decay Rates and Domain Dependence of a Coupled Wave-Heat System with Spatially Dependant Heat Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03980)
*Pascal Heymoß*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合波-热系统的长期行为，该系统由两个相邻Lipschitz域上的波方程和热方程通过公共界面耦合而成，热方程可含空间依赖系数。论文首先在任意域和系数下建立强渐近稳定性；其次利用C₀-半群理论和状态空间非正交分解方法，分析了经典解的非一致衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合波-热系统在数学物理中具有重要应用（如声-热交互、结构热弹性等），但其长期行为分析因界面耦合和空间依赖系数而具有挑战性。已有结果多限于常数系数或特定几何，本文旨在推广到更一般的Lipschitz域和空间依赖系数情形。

Method: (1) 利用闭包关系（closure relations）框架，将耦合系统的谱分析简化为单一波方程的谱分析，从而建立与域和系数无关的强渐近稳定性。(2) 借助C₀-半群理论和对状态空间的非正交分解，将问题归约为一个残留估计（residual estimate），该估计与热域及热系数无关，仅单调依赖于波域和界面结构，进而推导非一致衰减率。

Result: 将已知的非一致衰减率结果推广到空间依赖热系数情形：在无额外假设下获得对数衰减，在一维情形或几何控制条件（GCC）下获得多项式衰减。

Conclusion: 本文表明耦合波-热系统的能量衰减行为主要由波域几何和界面性质控制，而热域大小及热系数（在空间依赖下）不影响衰减率的本质阶数。这一结果为更广泛耦合PDE系统的稳定性分析提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study of the long-term behavior of a coupled wave-heat system. The system consists of a wave equation and a heat equation on two adjacent Lipschitz domains coupled by a common interface, with the heat equation being allowed to incorporate spatially dependant coefficients.
  We first establish strong asymptotic stability independent of the domains and coefficients. To this end, we employ the framework of closure relations, which reduces the spectral analysis of the coupled system to that of a wave equation.
  Secondly, we analyze non-uniform decay rates for classical solutions to the coupled system. Using methods from the theory of $C_0$-semigroups and a non-orthogonal decomposition of the state space, we reduce the problem to a residual estimate which is independent of the heat domain and heat coefficients, instead depending monotonically on the wave domain and the interface. With this, we are able to extend the known non-uniform decay rates to spatially dependent heat coefficients, yielding logarithmic decay under no assumptions, as well as polynomial decay in one dimensions or under the Geometric Control Condition.

</details>


### [189] [Caffarelli Estimates under Lipschitz Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04052)
*Maja Gwóźdź*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Brenier映射在边缘分布一阶摄动下的无维数微分估计。作者证明了在边缘分布为任意全局Lipschitz摄动的情况下，Caffarelli收缩定理的结论（即全局Lipschitz Brenier映射及Hessian估计）仍然成立，从而回答了Fathi、Mikulincer和Shenfeld提出的猜想。文中给出了高斯测度情形下显式的上界常数渐近行为，并通过各向异性双边缘结果建立了矩阵Hessian界，对仿射摄动恢复出sharp的非交换Caffarelli张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caffarelli收缩定理在源势与目标势满足逐点Hessian比较时给出无维数的Lipschitz估计，但实际问题中边缘分布通常只受全局Lipschitz摄动而非严格的Hessian比较约束。作者希望在不添加额外假设的前提下，将此类估计推广到任意全局Lipschitz摄动情形，以回答Fathi-Mikulincer-Shenfeld猜想，并为高维最优传输问题提供无维数的正则性估计。

Method: 作者首先从高斯测度情形出发，对密度扰动B为全局Lipschitz函数的测度，建立Brenier映射的全局Lipschitz正则性及C^{1,1}的Brenier势能。关键工具是引入各向异性双边缘结果：基于分布曲率界D^2V⪯Q和D^2W⪰P，推导Brenier映射的矩阵Hessian估计，且常数保持本质梯度范围的方向几何。仿射摄动情形下该估计精确恢复非交换Caffarelli张量。通过上界常数C(0,L)的渐近分析和一维算例T_L的下界估计，佐证了结果的sharp性。

Result: 证明了Brenier映射从标准高斯测度γ_d到密度为e^{-B}γ_d的测度具有全局Lipschitz代表元，Lipschitz常数仅依赖于L；Brenier势属于C^{1,1}(R^d)且满足无维数双侧Hessian界。上界常数满足log C(0,L)=4L^2+log L+O(1)，一维例子给出下界log Lip(T_L)≥L^2/2。各向异性双边缘框架下建立了矩阵Hessian界，对仿射摄动精确恢复非交换Caffarelli张量，并进一步得到高斯摄动的逐点位移界和紧致高斯混合的谱Hessian估计。

Conclusion: 作者证实了Caffarelli型收缩估计在任意全局Lipschitz边缘摄动下成立，无需额外假设，从而正面回答了Fathi-Mikulincer-Shenfeld猜想。文中建立的各向异性框架不仅统一了已有的张量结果，其所保持的方向几何结构也为更广泛的最优传输正则性研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study dimension-free differential estimates for Brenier maps under first-order perturbations of the two marginals. Caffarelli's contraction theorem yields such estimates when the source and target potentials satisfy a pointwise Hessian comparison. We show that the same conclusion remains true under arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of both marginals, without additional assumptions. In particular, this answers the conjecture due to Fathi, Mikulincer, and Shenfeld.
  More precisely, let $d\ge1$, $L\ge0$, and let $B:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be globally $L$-Lipschitz. We prove that the Brenier map from the standard Gaussian measure $γ_d$ to the probability measure proportional to $\mathrm{e}^{-B}γ_d$ has a globally Lipschitz representative whose bound depends only on $L$. The Brenier potential also belongs to $C^{1,1}(\mathbb{R}^d)$ and satisfies dimension-free two-sided Hessian bounds. Let $\mathfrak{C}(0,L)$ denote the upper-bound constant, then \[ \log\mathfrak{C}(0,L)=4L^2+\log L+\mathcal{O}(1) \qquad(L\to\infty), \] and a one-dimensional example with Brenier map $T_L$ satisfies $\log\operatorname{Lip}(T_L)\ge L^2/2$.
  We deduce this Gaussian estimate from an anisotropic two-marginal result. Let $V,W:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be potentials, and let $Q,P$ be positive-definite matrices. Using the distributional curvature bounds $D^2V\preceq Q$ and $D^2W\succeq P$, we establish matrix Hessian bounds for Brenier maps between arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of the reference marginals. Most importantly, the constants keep the directional geometry of the essential gradient ranges. For affine perturbations, our estimates recover the sharp noncommuting Caffarelli tensor. We further obtain pointwise displacement bounds for Gaussian perturbations and spectral Hessian estimates for compact Gaussian mixtures.

</details>


### [190] [Smooth autonomous fast dynamo action on the three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04153)
*Michele Coti Zelati,Massimo Sorella,David Villringer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文解决了Zeldovich与Sakharov提出的Fast Dynamo猜想，在三维环面$\mathbb{T}^3$上构造了一个非空的$C^k$-开族光滑无散速度场，这些场能够产生快速发电机效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Arnold问题集中记载的Zeldovich-Sakharov Fast Dynamo猜想，即证明在光滑自治无散速度场中存在非空开族能够产生快速发电机，而非仅依赖特殊构造或非自治情形。

Method: 分两步构造：(1) 通过拉伸-折叠-剪切机制构造一族光滑、时间周期的快速发电机；(2) 利用全局Poincaré截面与适当规定的回归时间函数，将周期动力学嵌入光滑自治流。证明过程先在各向异性分布Banach空间上分离理想感应算子的不稳定分布特征模，再证明该谱不稳定性在奇异摄动$\varepsilon\Delta$下持续存在。

Result: 所得估计关于$\varepsilon\to0$一致成立，且对速度场的$C^k$扰动稳定，最终得到光滑快速发电机向量场的开集，从而在正问题上完全解决了Fast Dynamo猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着快速发电机理论的一个重要突破，以严格的数学构造证明了快速发电机向量场在光滑自治无散场空间中并非孤立或病态对象，而构成一个开集，具有结构稳定性。

Abstract: We construct a nonempty $C^k$-open family, for some $k\in\mathbb{N}$, of smooth, autonomous, divergence-free velocity fields on $\mathbb{T}^3$ that generate fast dynamos. This resolves the Fast Dynamo Conjecture of Zeldovich and Sakharov, as recorded in Arnold's book of problems. We first construct a family of smooth, time-periodic fast dynamos via a stretch-fold-shear mechanism that applies to a large class of velocity fields. We then realize the associated period dynamics within a smooth autonomous flow using a global Poincaré section and a suitably prescribed return-time function. In both settings, the proof first isolates unstable distributional eigenmodes for the ideal induction operator on an anisotropic Banach space of distributions and then shows that this spectral instability persists under the singular perturbation $\varepsilon Δ$. The resulting estimates are uniform as $\varepsilon\to0$ and stable under $C^k$ perturbations of the velocity field, yielding an open set of smooth fast dynamo vector fields.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

### [191] [Finite-source filtering and Doppler modulation of infragravity waves generated by breakpoint forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03300)
*Hedi Midouni,Stephanie Contardo,Jaak Monbaliu,Tomohiro Suzuki*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文从理论推导与数值模拟两个层面，重新审视破波区中由破波强迫产生的次重力波（infragravity waves）。作者将波致强迫分解为“移动破波点”与“束缚波”两部分，并把破波强迫视为一个有限扩展、随时间演化的源，通过其强度质心、宽度与形状加以描述。研究发现：源的有限横向尺度会造成空间干涉，抑制高频次重力波响应，且源越宽衰减越向低频移动；源向岸/离岸迁移则产生方向性多普勒频移。破波深度同时控制强迫向水面高程的转化及传播相位速度。数值实验在多种平面海滩坡度上验证了上述预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对破波点强迫产生次重力波的解释大多依赖理想化的“移动破波点”图像，假设破波与局地饱和关系紧密绑定，因而无法回答真实破波区的有限空间范围与时空演变如何控制辐射波这一问题。作者希望建立更真实、可解析的描述框架以填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先对波致强迫做局地分解，在同一个演化的短波场中将“破波点强迫”与“束缚波强迫”两种贡献分离；随后将破波强迫场表征为一个有限、演化的源，用其积分强度、质心、离岸宽度及形状四个量刻画。基于此表征推导有限源宽度导致的空间干涉效应以及源迁移引起的方向性多普勒频移，并用解析表达式连接破波深度与转换效率、传播相位速度。最后通过不同平面海滩坡度下的受控数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 理论预测与数值实验结果一致：更宽的破波源足迹使高频频段的衰减在向岸、离岸两个分支上都更强；实际诊断得到的迁移历史（破波在向岸运动中最强）会部分抵消向岸侧的衰减并加重离岸侧的衰减。这证实了源的有限宽度、迁移与破波深度共同决定次重力波的辐射特征。

Conclusion: 论文指出，对次重力波辐射的准确刻画需超越理想化移动破波点图像，转而考虑破波区作为有限、演化源的影响：有限源宽度引起的高频抑制、源迁移带来的方向性多普勒位移以及破波深度对转换与相位速度的控制，共同塑造了近岸次重力波频谱的跨岸结构。这一框架为解释和预测近岸水位变率提供了更为完整的理论基础。

Abstract: Infragravity waves generated in the surf zone contribute substantially to nearshore water-level variability, with group-scale variations in wave breaking generating free long waves through breakpoint forcing. Existing interpretations of this mechanism rely largely on an idealised moving-breakpoint picture in which breaking is tied to a local saturation relation, leaving unresolved how the finite extent and evolution of a realistic breaking region control the radiated waves.
  We first derive a local decomposition of the wave-induced forcing into breakpoint and bound wave forcing, separating the two contributions within the same evolving short-wave field. We then describe the breakpoint-forcing field as a finite, evolving source through its integrated strength, centroid, cross-shore width, and shape. This representation shows that finite source extent produces spatial interference, as phase differences between contributions emitted from different positions attenuate the high-frequency part of the infragravity response and shift this attenuation toward lower frequencies as the source widens. Source migration produces a directional Doppler shift by modifying the arrival times of successive emissions, with shoreward motion compressing arrivals at a shoreward observer and stretching them at a seaward observer. Breaking depth controls both the conversion of this forcing into surface elevation and the celerities governing these propagation-time effects. Controlled numerical experiments across a range of plane-beach slopes reproduce the predicted signatures: broader source footprints strengthen upper-band attenuation on both branches. The diagnosed migration history, with breaking strongest during shoreward motion, partly offsets that attenuation shoreward and reinforces it seaward.

</details>


### [192] [Cascade coalescence dynamically sustains bubble retention near gas-evolving surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03387)
*Tao Wu,Bo Liu,Haohao Hao,Xuehua Zhang,Fang Yuan,Huanshu Tan,Qiang Yang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究发现，在高气体通量条件下气泡滞留于固体表面并非源于浮力不足，而是级联聚并机制所致：上升气泡与表面附着的小气泡合并时，界面不对称回缩产生非抵消粘性应力，形成向下的净冲量，使气泡轨迹反常反转并加速朝向基底。重复的级联聚并将瞬时冲量累积为稳定的时均保持力，其量级可达准静态极限的三到四个数量级以上，从而揭示了气泡聚并是主导高气体通量下气泡动态滞留的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高电流密度水电解和沸腾热管理等清洁能源关键技术中，气泡从固体表面脱离对传热、传质和电荷传输至关重要。然而在高气体通量下，气泡尽管受到巨大浮力仍被滞留在活性表面，严重限制传质并增加能量损失。理解这一反常滞留现象的物理机制是优化相关技术效率的关键。

Method: 本研究采用多手段相结合的方法：(1) 微电极周围的高速光学观测，直接捕捉气泡的聚并与运动轨迹变化；(2) 直接数值模拟（DNS）以解析聚并过程中的流场细节；(3) 尺度分析推导控制气泡行为的物理规律。

Result: 实验观察到当上升气泡与更小的表面附着后继气泡合并时，其轨迹发生突然逆转，以接近上升速度两个数量级的加速度冲向基底。直接数值模拟与尺度分析表明，合并时的不对称界面回缩产生无法相互抵消的粘性应力，形成指向较小气泡的净向下冲量。重复的级联聚并可将这些瞬时冲量累积成为一种稳定的时均滞留力，其强度可抗衡超出准静态极限三到四个数量级的浮力。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了气泡聚并作为此前未被认识的一种机制，能够在高气体通量条件下动态维持气泡在固体表面的滞留。这一发现挑战了传统上仅以浮力-表面力平衡理解气泡脱离的范式，为高电流密度电解与沸腾热管理系统的优化设计提供了新的物理基础。

Abstract: Bubble detachment from solid surfaces governs heat, mass, and charge transport across technologies vital to clean energy, including high-current-density water electrolysis and boiling thermal management. At high gas fluxes, however, bubbles remain trapped at active surfaces despite immense buoyancy, severely restricting mass transfer and increasing energy losses. Here, we show that this unexpected surface retention originates from cascade coalescence between unequal-sized bubbles. High-speed observations around microelectrodes demonstrate that when a rising bubble merges with a smaller surface-attached successor, its trajectory abruptly reverses, accelerating toward the substrate at nearly two orders of magnitude above its rising speed. Direct numerical simulations and scaling analysis reveal that asymmetric interfacial retraction during merging generates non-canceling viscous stresses, producing a net downward impulse toward the smaller bubble. Repeated cascade coalescence events accumulate these transient impulses into a steady, time-averaged retaining force capable of opposing buoyancy three to four orders of magnitude beyond quasistatic limits. Our findings establish bubble coalescence as a previously unrecognized mechanism that dynamically sustains bubble retention under high gas flux.

</details>


### [193] [Interface-resolved simulations of boiling heat transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03396)
*Alessio Roccon,Luca Brandt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了沸腾流动中界面解析模拟的最新进展，涵盖流场与温度场控制方程、界面热质传递耦合策略以及成核、微液层蒸发和接触线动力学等小尺度物理的建模方法。核心观点是：界面解析沸腾模拟在流体动力学层面已实现解析，但在微观层面仍依赖模型，其预测精度取决于亚网格闭合模型的准确性，而非仅取决于水动力学求解器的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沸腾传热在许多需要耗散大热通量的应用中至关重要，但其涉及宽范围耦合的多尺度输运现象，数值精确预测一直是一个长期挑战。随着数值格式改进、热力学与可压缩性建模的完善及算力增长，界面解析模拟方法取得了显著进展，亟需对该领域的现状进行系统梳理与总结。

Method: 本文通过综述的方式，系统考察了界面解析沸腾模拟的多个关键方面：液汽界面显式或隐式描述的不同流场与温度场控制方程公式；界面处热量与质量传递的耦合策略；成核、微液层蒸发和接触线动力学等小尺度物理的现有建模方法；并基于近期高保真模拟结果进行分析与归纳。

Result: 核心发现是界面解析沸腾模拟"水动力学已解析、微观仍需模型"：其预测精度对成核、微液层和接触线物理的亚网格闭合模型的依赖程度不亚于水动力求解器的精度。文章据此讨论了现有方法的优势与局限。

Conclusion: 文章在总结界面解析沸腾模拟方法优势与不足的基础上，指出了沸腾传热精确描述方面仍存在的开放挑战，为该领域未来的研究方向提供了清晰的参考框架。

Abstract: Boiling heat transfer underpins many applications where large heat fluxes must be dissipated. Boiling is characterized by a wide range of coupled multiscale transport phenomena, making its accurate numerical prediction a long-standing challenge. In this context, interface-resolved simulation approaches - where the liquid-vapor interface is either explicitly or implicitly described - have undergone significant advancements, driven by improved numerical formulations, more complete thermodynamic and compressibility modeling, and the growing availability of computational resources. Here, we provide a comprehensive overview of the state of the art in interface-resolved simulations of boiling flows. We examine the different formulations governing the flow and temperature field, strategies for coupling heat and mass transfer at the interface, and current modeling approaches for small-scale physics such as nucleation, microlayer evaporation, and contact-line dynamics. By analyzing insights from recent high-fidelity simulations, a central observation is that interface-resolved simulations of boiling are hydrodynamically resolved but microscopically modeled: their predictive accuracy depends on the sub-grid closures for nucleation, microlayer, and contact-line physics no less than on the fidelity of the hydrodynamic solver. We discuss the strengths and limitations of available methods on this basis, together with the open challenges in the accurate description of boiling heat transfer.

</details>


### [194] [A conservative coupling method of sharp-interface and multi-species model for compressible reacting gas-liquid flows with phase change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03509)
*Jiaxi Song,Yunzhang Tian,Shucheng Pan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种用于可压缩两相多组分流动（含相变和化学反应）的守恒性锐界面-扩散界面耦合方法。液相-气相界面采用锐界面模型，气相组分输运和化学反应采用扩散界面模型，通过多组分相变黎曼问题获得界面通量实现守恒耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩多相多组分流动中同时存在相变和化学反应，需要正确处理界面守恒、质量传递和能量耦合，单一界面模型难以同时精确描述相界面和气体组分输运，需要一种耦合策略。

Method: 提出锐界面-扩散界面耦合框架：锐界面描述液气界面，扩散界面处理气相组分；将单组分四波黎曼求解器扩展至多组分气体混合物，通过修正界面能量跳跃条件实现；界面传质仅限可凝蒸气组分，界面能量跳跃条件和气体混合物能量交换通量基于相变蒸气组分的内能而非混合物内能构建；基于物种选择性能量耦合构造近似的保持四波结构的黎曼求解器，避免精确解中多维非线性求根。

Result: 通过脉冲蒸发与冷凝、反应性铝汽化、激波-液滴相互作用和爆轰-液滴相互作用等系列数值测试验证，数值结果与参考解和基准数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在保持守恒界面耦合的同时，正确分辨可压缩多组分多相流动中相变和化学反应的影响，具有较高的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, a conservative sharp-interface and diffuse-interface coupling method is developed for compressible two-phase multi-species flows with phase change and chemical reactions. The liquid--gas interface is represented by a sharp-interface model, whereas a diffuse-interface model treats the transport and chemical reactions of gas-phase species. Conservation is enforced by coupling the two phases through interfacial fluxes obtained from a multi-species phase-change Riemann problem. The original single-species four-wave Riemann solver is extended to multi-species gas mixtures by modifying the interfacial energy jump condition. Interfacial mass transfer is restricted to the condensable vapor species. Accordingly, both the interfacial energy jump condition and the gas-mixture energy-exchange flux are constructed using the internal energy of the phase-changing vapor species rather than the mixture internal energy. With this species-selective energy coupling, an approximate multi-species Riemann solver is constructed that retains the four-wave structure while avoiding the multidimensional nonlinear root-finding required by the exact solution. A series of numerical tests, including impulsive evaporation and condensation, reacting aluminum vaporization, shock-droplet interaction, and detonation-droplet interaction, are performed to assess the accuracy and robustness of the method. The numerical results agree well with reference solutions and benchmark data, demonstrating that the present method resolves the effects of phase change and chemical reactions in compressible multi-species multiphase flows while preserving conservative interfacial coupling.

</details>


### [195] [Mach-number-dependent dissipative anomaly in isothermal compressible turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03945)
*Shadab Alam,Georgy Zinchenko,Christoph Federrath,Jörg Schumacher*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高达2048^3分辨率的三维直接数值模拟，研究了等温可压缩均匀各向同性湍流中耗散反常的存在性。研究发现总动能耗散率及其有旋和无散分量随雷诺数增大趋于有限渐近值，且归一化平均耗散率在亚声速/跨声速（M_rms≲1）与高声速（M_rms≥3）区间形成两条不同分支。通过三种互补方法（耗散率平衡方程分析、Duchon-Robert框架、强耗散区域几何表征）提供了一致证据支持可压缩湍流中耗散反常的存在，但无法明确区分其为弱或强反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究等温可压缩均匀各向同性湍流中是否存在耗散反常——即在无粘极限下动能耗散率是否保持有限非零的经典问题。此前研究多在不可压缩湍流中验证Kolmogorov的耗散反常理论，但可压缩效应对耗散反常的存在性及其强弱特征影响尚不明确，需要高分辨率数值模拟进行系统研究。

Method: 采用高达2048^3网格点的三维高分辨率直接数值模拟（DNS），对受无散力驱动的等温可压缩均匀各向同性湍流进行分析。通过跨雷诺数的系统模拟，测量总动能耗散率及其有旋（solenoidal）和无散（dilatational）分量，并结合三种互补方法：(i)基于耗散率平衡方程及其各产生项的机制分析；(ii)扩展至可压缩流的Duchon-Robert框架来研究反常耗散的前兆信号；(iii)对强耗散区域的几何结构进行表征。

Result: 总动能耗散率及其两个分量随雷诺数增大趋于有限渐近值。归一化平均耗散率在M_rms≲1和M_rms≥3两个马赫数区间呈现两条不同分支，该两分支行为对总动能耗散最显著。有旋和无散分量对Re的依赖还受积分尺度和均方根速度选法影响。尽管高达2048^3的分辨率，可达到的雷诺数仍不足以在统计上明确区分弱耗散反常与强耗散反常。

Conclusion: 三种互补分析方法提供了一致的证据支持等温可压缩湍流中存在耗散反常现象，但其精确的弱或强特征尚未得到定论。高马赫数下耗散率出现不同于亚/跨声速的分支行为，暗示可压缩性对耗散反常的普适性有重要影响，或需更高雷诺数或更精细的统计方法才能最终判定其强弱性质。

Abstract: Using a comprehensive set of three-dimensional, high-resolution direct numerical simulations, we investigate the existence of a dissipative anomaly in isothermal, homogeneous, isotropic compressible turbulence driven by solenoidal forcing. We find that the total kinetic-energy dissipation rate, as well as its solenoidal and dilatational components, approaches finite asymptotic values with increasing Reynolds number $Re$. The normalized mean dissipation rates collapse onto two distinct branches: one corresponding to the subsonic and transonic regimes, with root-mean-square Mach numbers ($M_{\rm rms}\lesssim 1$), and another to the highly supersonic regime, with ($M_{\rm rms}\ge 3$). This two-branch Mach-number dependence is most pronounced for the total kinetic-energy dissipation. For the solenoidal and dilatational dissipation rate components, the dependence on $Re$ depends in addition on the specific choice of the integral scale and root-mean-square velocity. Despite grid resolutions of up to $2048^3$ points the Reynolds numbers accessible are not sufficiently large to distinguish conclusively between a weak and a strong dissipative anomaly. We substantiate these findings using three complementary approaches: (i) a detailed analysis of the mechanisms responsible for dissipation generation, based on the corresponding dissipation-rate balance equations and their individual production terms; (ii) an investigation of precursors of anomalous dissipation using the Duchon--Robert framework extended to compressible flows; and (iii) a geometrical characterization of regions of intense dissipation. Taken together, these analyses provide consistent evidence for the existence of a dissipative anomaly in isothermal compressible turbulence, while leaving its precise weak or strong character unresolved.

</details>


### [196] [How do flapping avian wing exhibit superior aerodynamic performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03957)
*Dilip Thakur,Muhammad Saif Ullah Khalid*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究利用自研尖锐界面浸没边界求解器VorteXdyn，对仿鹰扑翼在雷诺数2500-10000和斯特劳哈尔数0.18-0.27范围内的非定常气动性能与涡动力学进行了数值研究。通过三种翼构型（简化翼、带锯齿羽毛缘翼、多层真实羽毛翼）对比，发现几何逼真度的增加会改变气动力产生、多个前缘涡的形成演化、涡-涡及涡-翼相互作用以及尾迹拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解鸟类羽毛形态在三维扑翼飞行中的气动作用，为设计高效仿生扑翼微型飞行器（MAV）提供理论依据。尽管已有大量扑翼空气动力学研究，但羽毛结构（如锯齿和多层羽片）对气动力和前缘涡动力学的具体影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用自研尖锐界面浸没边界求解器VorteXdyn，基于NACA 4312翼型构建鹰仿生体翼模型。系统改变雷诺数（2500、5000、10000）和斯特劳哈尔数（0.18、0.225、0.27），并比较三种翼构型：无羽毛结构简化翼、后缘带锯齿羽毛结构的翼、包含初级/次级/再生羽毛的多层真实几何翼。通过升阻力系数时间历程和时均值量化气动性能，并通过前缘涡的展向发展、环量、特征尺寸和持续性表征涡动力学。

Result: 结果表明，几何逼真度的提升显著改变气动力产生与多个前缘涡（LEV）的形成和演化过程，影响前缘涡从翼根到翼尖的展向发展及其与翼尖涡的相互作用，同时改变下拍过程中的尾迹演化与拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 羽毛形态结构对三维扑翼飞行的气动性能和涡动力学具有重要影响，研究结果可为高效仿生扑翼微型飞行器的气动设计提供启示。

Abstract: This work investigates the unsteady aerodynamic performance and vortex dynamics of avian-inspired flapping wings using our in-house sharp-interface immersed-boundary solver, VorteXdyn. A falcon-inspired body-wing model based on NACA 4312 profile is employed to examine aerodynamic force generation and vortex evolution during steady forward flapping flight at Reynolds numbers of 2,500, 5000, and 10000 and Strouhal numbers of 0.18, 0.225, and 0.27. The influence of physiologies is examined using three wing configurations: a simplified wing without distinct feather structures, a wing incorporating feather-like structures (serrations) along the trailing edge, and a geometrically detailed wing incorporating multiple feather layers consisting of primary, secondary, and median feathers over its span. The aerodynamic performance of these configurations is quantified using the temporal profiles and time-averaged characteristics of the lift and drag coefficients and the lift-to-drag ratio. The associated vortex dynamics are characterized through the formation and evolution of multiple leading-edge vortices (LEVs), their spanwise coherence, circulation, characteristic size, and persistence over the wings' surfaces. Particular emphasis is placed on the spanwise development of the LEVs from the root to the wingtip, their interactions with the tip vortices, and the resulting wake evolution during the downstroke. Our results demonstrate that increasing geometric fidelity modifies aerodynamic force production, the formation and evolution of multiple LEV structures, vortex-vortex and votex-wing interactions, and wake topology. These findings provide insight into the aerodynamic role of feather morphology in three-dimensional flapping flight for the aerodynamic design of efficient bio-inspired flapping-wing micro air vehicles.

</details>


### [197] [Analytic study of the continuous spectrum of three-dimensional weak shock layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03969)
*Vassilis Theofilis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文分析了弱可压缩激波层的三维线性稳定性，利用精确的Reynolds数无关的基流与线性稳定方程，证明了特征值问题在横向波数矢量旋转下具有协变性，并将三维失稳模式精确分解为二维可压缩面内声学-熵分支与一维纯螺线管涡量分支，得出与Squire经典定理类似但由横向波数代替Reynolds数起稳定作用的精确结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可压缩激波层稳定性分析缺乏精确、Reynolds数无关的解析框架，使三维扰动与二维扰动之间的稳定性关系难以严格刻画，阻碍了经典Squire定理在可压缩、Reynolds无关情形下的拓展。

Method: 通过将问题重标度到激波自身的自然粘性长度和时间尺度，得到Reynolds数无关的基流与线性稳定方程组；在横向速度分量为零、仅依赖激波法向坐标的基流假设下，证明特征值问题对横向波数矢量旋转的协变性，进而将三维本征模态分解为面内声学-熵分支与纯螺线管涡量分支两个独立的子算子。

Result: 三维激波层稳定性被精确分解为可压缩与涡量两个组成部分，并通过Grosch–Salwen远场伴随谱在机器精度内验证。两个解耦算子均携带随总横向波数增长的显式阻尼项，故三维倾斜扰动不弱于与其共享面内波数的二维扰动；涡量分支谱随波数平方成比例地远离虚轴。

Conclusion: 该结果是对Squire经典定理的精确、无Reynolds数类比：此处横向波数幅值充当了稳定化参数的角色，替代了经典定理中Reynolds数的作用，为三维可压缩激波层稳定性提供了严格的解析分解与验证。

Abstract: Three-dimensional linear stability of a weak, compressible shock layer is analyzed using exact Reynolds-number-independent forms of the governing base flow and linear stability equations, obtained by rescaling the problem onto the shock's own natural viscous length and time scales. For any base flow with zero transverse velocity components, depending on the shock-normal coordinate alone, the resulting eigenvalue problem is proved covariant under rotation of the transverse wavenumber vector: the eigenvalue depends on the transverse wavenumbers only through their magnitude, and every three-dimensional eigenmode, throughout the continuous spectrum, decouples exactly into two independent constituents. A two-dimensional, in-plane acoustic--entropy branch carries the disturbance's entire dilatation, pressure and thermodynamic coupling, and a one-dimensional, out-of-plane purely solenoidal vortical branch is governed by a single scalar shear-diffusion equation. Three-dimensional shock-layer stability is thus resolved exactly into its compressible and vortical constituents, confirmed to machine precision by the Grosch--Salwen far-field companion spectra. Both decoupled operators carry an explicit damping term growing with the total transverse wavenumber, so a three-dimensional, oblique disturbance is never less stable than the two-dimensional disturbance sharing its in-plane wavenumber alone; the vortical branch, whose spectrum is controlled directly by this term, is shown to retreat from the imaginary axis in close proportion to the wavenumber squared. This finding is an exact, Reynolds-free analogue of Squire's classical theorem, with the stabilizing role of Reynolds number played here by the transverse wavenumber magnitude.

</details>


### [198] [Dissipation scaling in wind turbine wakes exposed to free-stream turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03996)
*Martin Bourhis,Oliver R. H. Buxton*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究0.58m直径风力机尾流中湍动能耗散的性质，在多种高雷诺数来流湍流条件下发现：外尾流叶片尖部区域出现升高归一化耗散率的环形区域，该区域的Cε与√Re_D/Re_λ呈比例，表明耗散与惯性区能级间通量处于非平衡状态；该非平衡由大尺度间歇事件（尖端涡/剪切层动力学）驱动。尾流中心线呈经典Kolmogorov平衡或平衡非平衡态。高强度湍流下大尺度间歇被抑制，无法用单一湍流雷诺数描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解风力机尾流中湍动能耗散的机理对于风电场布局优化、下游湍流预测及载荷评估至关重要。已有研究对于耗散率在叶片尖部区域增强的原因、以及其与湍流间歇性的定量联系认识不足，特别是在不同强度与性质的自由来流湍流条件下，耗散是否处于非平衡状态缺乏系统的实验证据。

Method: 对直径D=0.58m的风力机模型尾流在不同"类型"（flavours）的高雷诺数自由来流湍流条件下进行实验测量，系统地比较低强度与高强度来流湍流工况。测量归一化耗散率Cε在尾流不同径向与流向位置的变化，并结合小尺度（ℓ≤λ）与大尺度（ℓ≥D）两个尺度的间歇性分析，检验Cε与Re_D和Re_λ的组合标度关系，揭示耗散与惯性区间尺度间能量通量的平衡状态。

Result: (1) 在低和中强度来流湍流下，外尾流出现环形的高Cε区域，与间歇性增强环重合（涵盖小尺度与大尺度）；(2) 叶片尖端区域Cε遵循Cε∝√Re_D/Re_λ标度，表明耗散与惯性区能级间通量不平衡；(3) 大尺度间歇事件（低波数扰动）源于持久存在的尖端涡与尖端剪切层动力学，需要有限时间向下级联到耗散尺度，引入不平衡；(4) 中心线处Cε近似恒定，呈经典Kolmogorov平衡或平衡的非平衡湍流；(5) 高强度来流湍流下尖端涡被侵蚀，大尺度间歇被抑制，无法用单一湍流Reynolds数刻画Cε。

Conclusion: 风力机尾流中耗散的非平衡本质与大尺度间歇事件密切相关：尖端涡/剪切层产生的大尺度扰动需经历有限时间的能量级联过程，导致耗散率滞后于能级间能量通量，形成非平衡耗散状态（Cε∝√Re_D/Re_λ）。这一机制在低/中来流湍流下于叶尖区域显著，而在中心线及高强度来流湍流下（尖端涡被抑制）不适用。结果表明风力机尾流中不同区域的耗散特性受控于不同物理机制，单一湍流雷诺数不足以统一描述所有工况，对未来尾流湍流模型提出了挑战。

Abstract: The nature of the dissipation of turbulent kinetic energy (TKE) is investigated experimentally in the wake of a diameter $D=0.58$m wind turbine exposed to several ``flavours'' of high-Reynolds-number free-stream turbulence (FST). For low- and moderate-intensity FST, an annular region of elevated normalised dissipation, $C_{\varepsilon}$, develops in the outer wake, coinciding with a ring of enhanced turbulence intermittency at both small ($\ell \leq λ$) and large ($\ell \geq D$) scales, where $λ$ is the Taylor microscale. In the blade-tip region, $C_{\varepsilon}$ scales with $\sqrt{Re_D}/Re_λ$, where $Re_D$ is a global Reynolds number and $Re_λ$ a local turbulent Reynolds number based on $λ$-a scaling indicative of dissipation being out of equilibrium with the inter-scale flux of TKE in the inertial range of the energy cascade. This non-equilibrium regime is interpreted in light of the observed intermittency : large-scale intermittent events (i.e., low-wavenumber perturbations/``kicks''), driven by persistent tip-vortex and tip-shear-layer dynamics under low-intensity FST, require a finite time to cascade down to the dissipative scales, thereby introducing an imbalance between the inter-scale energy flux and dissipation. At the wake centreline, by contrast, $C_{\varepsilon}$ remains approximately constant with streamwise distance, reflecting either classical Kolmogorov-type equilibrium turbulence or balanced non-equilibrium turbulence, with intermittency confined to the small scales. Under high-intensity FST, large-scale intermittency is suppressed, consistent with the erosion of tip-vortex structures, and no comparable scaling for $C_{\varepsilon}$ could be identified using a single turbulent Reynolds number for these cases, where two similarly intense streams of turbulence, but of different origins, are adjacent to one another.

</details>
